粮食问题是民众反对布尔什维克的主要原因，然而我看不出有什么顺应民心的政策可行。农民厌恶布尔什维克，是因为他们拿走的粮食太多；城市里厌恶布尔什维克，是因为他们拿进来的粮食太少。农民想要的是所谓的自由贸易，即取消对农产品的统制。如果采纳这项政策，城市面临的将不是仅仅饥饿与困苦，而是彻底的断粮绝户。认为农民对协约国（Entente）怀有任何敌意完全是一种误解。7 月 13 日的《每日新闻》（*Daily News*）在一篇其他方面均属优秀的社论中谈到了“既非共产党员也非布尔什维克的俄国农民，对整个盟国、尤其是对本国日益增长的仇恨”。典型的俄国农民从未听说过盟国或这个国家；他不知道存在封锁这回事；他所知道的一切就是：他过去拥有六头奶牛，但政府为了接济更贫困的农民而将他的奶牛削减到只剩一头，而且政府以极低的价格征收他的粮食（除了他自己一家所需的部分）。这些行为背后的理由他毫无兴趣，因为他的眼界仅限于自己的村庄。在惊人的程度上，每一个村庄都是一个独立的单元。只要政府能够获得其所需的粮食和兵源，它就不加干涉，听任古老的乡村村社共产主义保留原样，这种共产主义与布尔什维主义（Bolshevism）有着天壤之别，且完全依赖于一种非常原始的文化发展阶段。

===Original===

The Government represents the interests of the urban and industrial population, and is, as it were, encamped amid a peasant nation, with whom its relations are rather diplomatic and military than governmental in the ordinary sense. The economic situation, as in Central Europe, is favourable to the country and unfavourable to the towns. If Russia were governed democratically, according to the will of the majority, the inhabitants of Moscow and Petrograd would die of starvation. As it is, Moscow and Petrograd just manage to live, by having the whole civil and military power of the State devoted to their needs. Russia affords the curious spectacle of a vast and powerful Empire, prosperous at the periphery, but faced with dire want at the centre. Those who have least prosperity have most power; and it is only through their excess of power that they are enabled to live at all. The situation is due at bottom to two facts: that almost the whole industrial energies of the population have had to be devoted to war, and that the peasants do not appreciate the importance of the war or the fact of the blockade.
It is futile to blame the Bolsheviks for an unpleasant and difficult situation which it has been impossible for them to avoid. Their problem is only soluble in one of two ways: by the cessation of the war and the blockade, which would enable them to supply the peasants with the goods they need in exchange for food; or by the gradual development of an independent Russian industry. This latter method would be slow, and would involve terrible hardships, but some of the ablest men in the Government believe it to be possible if peace cannot be achieved. If we force this method upon Russia by the refusal of peace and trade, we shall forfeit the only inducement we can hold out for friendly relations; we shall render the Soviet State unassailable and completely free to pursue the policy of promoting revolution everywhere. But the industrial problem is a large subject, which has been already discussed in Chapter VI.

===Chinese===

政府代表着城市和工业人口的利益，它仿佛是扎营在一个农民国度之中，它与农民的关系与其说是通常意义上的政府治理，倒不如说是外交与军事性质的。经济局势如同在中欧一样，有利于乡村而不利于城市。如果俄国按照多数人的意愿实行民主治理，莫斯科和彼得格勒的居民将会饿死。而在目前的情况下，莫斯科和彼得格勒勉强维持生存，依靠的是将国家的全部民政和军事权力用于满足其需求。俄国呈现出一种奇特的景象：一个庞大而强大的帝国，在边缘地带繁荣昌盛，在核心地带却面临着极度的匮乏。那些最不繁荣的人掌握着最大的权力；而唯有通过其过度的权力，他们才能勉强生存下去。这一局面归根结底是由于两个事实：几乎全体居民的全部工业精力都不得不用于战争，以及农民既不理解战争的重要性，也不了解封锁的事实。

为了布尔什维克无法避免的不快和困境而去责难他们，是徒劳无益的。他们的问题唯有两种解决途径：要么停止战争和封锁，从而使他们能够向农民提供换取粮食所需的商品；要么逐步发展出独立的俄国工业。后一种方法将是缓慢的，并会伴随着可怕的艰难困苦，但政府中一些最有才干的人相信，如果无法实现和平，这种方法是可行的。如果我们通过拒绝和平与贸易而将这种方法强加给俄国，我们将丧失我们所能用来维系友好关系的唯一筹码；我们将使苏维埃国家变得坚不可摧，并且能够完全自由地在各地推行输出革命的政策。但工业问题是一个宏大的课题，已在第六章中作了探讨。

## IX International Policy / IX 国际政策
===Original===

In the course of these chapters, I have had occasion to mention disagreeable features of the Bolshevik regime. But it must always be remembered that these are chiefly due to the fact that the industrial life of Russia has been paralysed except as ministering to the wants of the Army, and that the Government has had to wage a bitter and doubtful civil and external war, involving the constant menace of domestic enemies. Harshness, espionage, and a curtailment of liberty result unavoidably from these difficulties. I have no doubt whatever that the sole cure for the evils from which Russia is suffering is peace and trade. Peace and trade would put an end to the hostility of the peasants, and would at once enable the Government to depend upon popularity rather than force. The character of the Government would alter rapidly under such conditions. Industrial conscription, which is now rigidly enforced, would become unnecessary. Those who desire a more liberal spirit would be able to make their voices heard without the feeling that they were assisting reaction and the national enemies. The food difficulties would cease, and with them the need for an autocratic system in the towns.
It must not be assumed, as is common with opponents of Bolshevism, that any other Government could easily be established in Russia. I think everyone who has been in Russia recently is convinced that the existing Government is stable. It may undergo internal developments, and might easily, but for Lenin, become a Bonapartist military autocracy. But this would be a change from within﻿—not perhaps a very great change﻿—and would probably do little to alter the economic system. From what I saw of the Russian character and of the opposition parties, I became persuaded that Russia is not ready for any form of democracy, and needs a strong Government. The Bolsheviks represent themselves as the Allies of Western advanced Socialism, and from this point of view they are open to grave criticism. For their international programme there is, to my mind, nothing to be said. But as a national Government, stripped of their camouflage, regarded as the successors of Peter the Great, they are performing a necessary though unamiable task. They are introducing, as far as they can, American efficiency among a lazy and undisciplined population.

===Chinese===

在这些章节中，我有机会提及了布尔什维克政权令人不快的种种特征。但必须始终记住的是，这些特征主要是由于俄国的工业生活除了服务于军队的需求之外已陷入瘫痪，而且政府不得不进行一场胜负未卜、艰难激烈的国内和对外战争，伴随着国内敌人的持续威胁。严酷、间谍活动以及对自由的剥夺，是这些困境下不可避免的结果。我毫不怀疑，医治俄国所承受之苦难的唯一良方就是和平与贸易。和平与贸易将终结农民的敌对情绪，并将立即使政府能够依靠民心而非武力进行统治。在这样的条件下，政府的性质将迅速发生改变。如今严格推行的工业劳动征用制将变得毫无必要。那些渴望更为自由主义精神的人将能够表达自己的声音，而不会觉得自己是在协助反动派和国家的敌人。粮食困难将不复存在，随之而来的是城市中专制体制的必要性也将消失。

不能像布尔什维主义（Bolshevism）的反对者通常所做的那样，假定在俄国可以轻易建立起任何其他政府。我认为最近去过俄国的每一个人都深信现存政府是稳固的。它可能会经历内部演变，若不是因为列宁（Lenin），它很容易变成一种波拿巴主义的军事独裁政权。但这将是一种来自内部的改变——或许不是很大的改变——而且可能对改变经济体制起不到什么作用。从我对俄国人民性格以及反对党的观察来看，我开始相信俄国尚未准备好迎接任何形式的民主，它需要一个强有力的政府。布尔什维克将自己描绘为西方先进社会主义的盟友，从这一角度来看，他们理应受到严厉的批评。对于他们的国际纲领，依我之见，毫无可取之处。但作为一个国家政府，褪去其伪装，被视作彼得大帝的继承者，他们正在履行一项必要虽不讨喜的任务。他们正在懒散且缺乏纪律的人口中尽可能地推行美国的效率。

===Original===

They are preparing to develop the natural resources of their country by the methods of State Socialism, for which, in Russia, there is much to be said. In the Army they are abolishing illiteracy, and if they had peace they would do great things for education everywhere.
But if we continue to refuse peace and trade, I do not think the Bolsheviks will go under. Russia will endure great hardships, in the years to come as before. But the Russians are inured to misery as no Western nation is; they can live and work under conditions which we should find intolerable. The Government will be driven more and more, from mere self-preservation, into a policy of imperialism. The Entente has been doing everything to expose Germany to a Russian invasion of arms and leaflets, by allowing Poland to engage in war and compelling Germany to disarm. All Asia lies open to Bolshevik ambitions. Almost the whole of the former Russian Empire in Asia is quite firmly in their grasp. Trains are running at a reasonable speed to Turkestan, and I saw cotton from there being loaded on to Volga steamers. In Persia and Turkey, revolts are taking place, with Bolshevik support. It is only a question of a few years before India will be in touch with the Red Army. If we continue to antagonize the Bolsheviks, I do not see what force exists that can prevent them from acquiring the whole of Asia within ten years.
The Russian Government is not yet definitely imperialistic in spirit, and would still prefer peace to conquest. The country is weary of war and denuded of goods. But if the Western Powers insist upon war, another spirit, which is already beginning to show itself, will become dominant. Conquest will be the only alternative to submission. Asiatic conquest will not be difficult. But for us, from the imperialist standpoint, it will mean utter ruin. And for the Continent it will mean revolutions, civil wars, economic cataclysms. The policy of crushing Bolshevism by force was always foolish and criminal; it has now become impossible and fraught with disaster. Our own Government, it would seem, have begun to realize the dangers, but apparently they do not realize them sufficiently to enforce their view against opposition.

===Chinese===

他们正准备通过国家社会主义的方法来开发本国的自然资源，在俄国，这种方法有许多值得称道之处。在军队中，他们正在扫除文盲，如果获得和平，他们将在各地的教育事业中大有作为。

但如果我们继续拒绝和平与贸易，我不认为布尔什维克会垮台。在未来的岁月里，俄国将一如既往地承受巨大的艰难困苦。但俄国人早已习惯了苦难，这是任何西方国家所未曾经历的；他们能够在我们认为无法忍受的条件下生活和劳作。纯粹出于自保，政府将被迫越来越滑向帝国主义政策。协约国（Entente）通过纵容波兰卷入战争并强迫德国解除武装，一直在竭尽所能使德国面临俄国武装与传单的入侵。整个亚洲都向布尔什维克的野心敞开大门。前俄罗斯帝国在亚洲的几乎全部领土都牢牢掌握在他们手中。前往土耳其斯坦的列车正以合理的速度运行，我看到来自那里的棉花正被装上伏尔加河的轮船。在波斯和土耳其，在布尔什维克的支持下，暴动正在发生。印度与红军（Red Army）产生接触只是几年内的问题。如果我们继续同布尔什维克对抗，我看不出有什么力量能够阻止他们在十年之内拿下整个亚洲。

俄国政府在精神上尚未明确具有帝国主义色彩，与征服相比它依然更倾向于和平。这个国家对战争感到厌倦，且物资匮乏。但如果西方列强坚持战争，另一种已经初露端倪的精神将占据主导地位。征服将成为屈服之外的唯一选择。征服亚洲并不困难。但对我们而言，从帝国主义的立场来看，这将意味着彻底的覆灭。而对欧洲大陆而言，这将意味着革命、内战与经济浩劫。企图用武力消灭布尔什维主义的政策始终是愚蠢且罪恶的；如今它已变得不可能实现，并且充满了灾难。我们自己的政府似乎已经开始认识到了这些危险，但显然他们并未充分认识到这些危险，以至于无法力排众议推行其观点。

===Original===

In the Theses presented to the Second Congress of the Third International (July 1920), there is a very interesting article by Lenin called “First Sketch of the Theses on National and Colonial Questions” (*Theses*, pp. 40﻿–﻿47). The following passages seemed to me particularly illuminating:﻿—

> The present world-situation in politics places on the order of the day the dictatorship of the proletariat; and all the events of world politics are inevitably concentrated round one centre of gravity: the struggle of the international bourgeoisie against the Soviet Republic, which inevitably groups round it, on the one hand the Sovietist movements of the advanced working men of all countries, on the other hand all the national movements of emancipation of colonies and oppressed nations which have been convinced by a bitter experience that there is no salvation for them except in the victory of the Soviet Government over world-imperialism.
> We cannot therefore any longer confine ourselves to recognizing and proclaiming the union of the workers of all countries. It is henceforth necessary to pursue the realization of the strictest union of all the national and colonial movements of emancipation with Soviet Russia, by giving to this union forms corresponding to the degree of evolution of the proletarian movement among the proletariat of each country, or of the democratic-bourgeois movement of emancipation among the workers and peasants of backward countries or backward nationalities.
> The federal principle appears to us as a transitory form towards the complete unity of the workers of all countries.

This is the formula for cooperation with Sinn Fein or with Egyptian and Indian nationalism. It is further defined later. In regard to backward countries, Lenin says, we must have in view:﻿—

> The necessity of the cooperation of all Communists in the democratic-bourgeois movement of emancipation in those countries.

Again:

> “The Communist International must conclude temporary alliances with the bourgeois democracy of backward countries, but must never fuse with it.” The class-conscious proletariat must “show itself particularly circumspect towards the survivals of national sentiment in countries long oppressed,” and must “consent to certain useful concessions.”

===Chinese===

在提交给第三国际（Third International）第二次代表大会（1920 年 7 月）的《纲领》（*Theses*）中，有一篇列宁撰写的极其有趣的文章，题为《民族和殖民地问题提纲初稿》（*Theses*，第 40—47 页）。以下段落在我看来尤具启发性：

> 当前世界政治局势将无产阶级专政（dictatorship of the proletariat）提上了日程；世界政治中的一切事件都不可避免地围绕着一个重力中心：国际资产阶级同苏维埃共和国的斗争，而苏维埃共和国不可避免地把两方面的力量聚集在自己周围：一方面是各国先进工人的苏维埃运动，另一方面是各殖民地和被压迫民族的一切民族解放运动，这些民族已从痛苦的经验中确信，除了苏维埃政权战胜世界帝国主义之外，他们别无生路。
> 因此，我们不能再仅仅局限于承认和宣告各国工人的联合。从此以后，必须致力于实现一切民族和殖民地解放运动与苏维埃俄国最紧密的联合，并使这种联合的形式与各国无产阶级中无产阶级运动的发展程度、或者落后国家或落后民族中工人和农民的资产阶级民主解放运动的发展程度相适应。
> 联邦制原则在我们看来是走向各国劳动者完全统一的过渡形式。

这是同新芬党（Sinn Fein）或同埃及与印度民族主义合作的公式。随后对此作了进一步的界定。关于落后国家，列宁说，我们必须着眼于：

> 一切共产党员必须协助这些国家的资产阶级民主解放运动。

再次：

> “共产国际必须同落后国家的资产阶级民主派结成临时联盟，但绝不能同它合并。”有阶级意识的（class-conscious）无产阶级必须“对长期受压迫国家中的民族情感残余表现出特别的谨慎态度”，并且必须“同意做出某些有益的让步”。

===Original===

The Asiatic policy of the Russian Government was adopted as a move against the British Empire, and as a method of inducing the British Government to make peace. It plays a larger part in the schemes of the leading Bolsheviks than is realized by the Labour Party in this country. Its method is not, for the present, to preach Communism, since the Persians and Hindus are considered scarcely ripe for the doctrines of Marx. It is nationalist movements that are supported by money and agitators from Moscow. The method of quasi-independent states under Bolshevik protection is well understood. It is obvious that this policy affords opportunities for imperialism, under the cover of propaganda, and there is no doubt that some among the Bolsheviks are fascinated by its imperialist aspect. The importance officially attached to the Eastern policy is illustrated by the fact that it was the subject of the concluding portion of Lenin’s speech to the recent Congress of the Third International (July 1920).
Bolshevism, like everything Russian, is partly Asiatic in character. One may distinguish two distinct trends, developing into two distinct policies. On the one side are the practical men, who wish to develop Russia industrially, to secure the gains of the Revolution nationally, to trade with the West, and gradually settle down into a more or less ordinary State. These men have on their side the fact of the economic exhaustion of Russia, the danger of ultimate revolt against Bolshevism if life continues to be as painful as it is at present, and the natural sentiment of humanity that wishes to relieve the sufferings of the people; also the fact that, if revolutions elsewhere produce a similar collapse of industry, they will make it impossible for Russia to receive the outside help which is urgently needed. In the early days, when the Government was weak, they had unchallenged control of policy, but success has made their position less secure.
On the other side there is a blend of two quite different aims: first, the desire to promote revolution in the Western nations, which is in line with Communist theory, and is also thought to be the only way of obtaining a really secure peace; secondly, the desire for Asiatic dominion, which is probably accompanied in the minds of some with dreams of sapphires and rubies and golden thrones and all the glories of their forefather Solomon.

===Chinese===

俄国政府的亚洲政策最初是作为针对大英帝国的策略而采纳的，并作为一种诱使英国政府缔和的手段。在布尔什维克主要领导人的计划中，这一政策所占的分量远比本国工党所认识到的要大得多。目前它的手段并不是宣传共产主义（Communism），因为波斯人和印度人被认为尚未成熟到能够接受马克思（Marx）的学说。由莫斯科（Moscow）提供资金和煽动人员所支持的，是民族主义运动。在布尔什维克保护下建立准独立国家的手法是广为人知的。显然，这项政策在宣传的掩护下为帝国主义提供了机会，毫无疑问，布尔什维克中的某些人正被其帝国主义面向所吸引。官方对东方政策赋予的重要性，由列宁在最近第三国际代表大会（1920 年 7 月）闭幕演说中将其作为总结主题这一事实得到了证明。

布尔什维主义如同俄国的一切事物一样，在性格上带有部分的亚洲色彩。人们可以区分出两种截然不同的倾向，并演化为两种截然不同的政策。一方面是务实派，他们希望在工业上发展俄国，在民族范围内巩固革命成果，与西方通商，并逐渐安顿为一个或多或少正常的国家。这些人拥有以下事实的支持：俄国在经济上的彻底疲竭、如果生活继续像目前这样痛苦则最终可能爆发反抗布尔什维主义的叛乱、希望减轻人民苦难的人道主义自然情感；以及这样一个事实：如果其他地方的革命造成类似的工业崩溃，将使俄国无法获得迫切需要的外部援助。在政府尚且虚弱的早期，他们无可争议地掌控着政策，但胜利却使他们的地位变得不那么稳固。

另一方面则是两种截然不同目标的混合：首先是渴望在西方国家推进革命，这符合共产主义理论，且被认为是获得真正稳固和平的唯一途径；其次是渴望建立亚洲统治，在某些人的心目中，这或许伴随着对蓝宝石、红宝石、黄金宝座以及他们祖先所罗门全部荣耀的梦想。

===Original===

This desire produces an unwillingness to abandon the Eastern policy, although it is realized that, until it is abandoned, peace with capitalist England is impossible. I do not know whether there are some to whom the thought occurs that if England were to embark on revolution we should become willing to abandon India to the Russians. But I am certain that the converse thought occurs, namely that, if India could be taken from us, the blow to imperialist feeling might lead us to revolution. In either case, the two policies, of revolution in the West and conquest (disguised as liberation of oppressed peoples) in the East, work in together, and dovetail into a strongly coherent whole.
Bolshevism as a social phenomenon is to be reckoned as a religion, not as an ordinary political movement. The important and effective mental attitudes to the world may be broadly divided into the religious and the scientific. The scientific attitude is tentative and piecemeal, believing what it finds evidence for, and no more. Since Galileo, the scientific attitude has proved itself increasingly capable of ascertaining important facts and laws, which are acknowledged by all competent people regardless of temperament or self-interest or political pressure. Almost all the progress in the world from the earliest times is attributable to science and the scientific temper; almost all the major ills are attributable to religion.
By a religion I mean a set of beliefs held as dogmas, dominating the conduct of life, going beyond or contrary to evidence, and inculcated by methods which are emotional or authoritarian, not intellectual. By this definition, Bolshevism is a religion: that its dogmas go beyond or contrary to evidence, I shall try to prove in what follows. Those who accept Bolshevism become impervious to scientific evidence, and commit intellectual suicide. Even if all the doctrines of Bolshevism were true, this would still be the case, since no unbiased examination of them is tolerated. One who believes, as I do, that the free intellect is the chief engine of human progress, cannot but be fundamentally opposed to Bolshevism, as much as to the Church of Rome.
Among religions, Bolshevism is to be reckoned with Mohammedanism rather than with Christianity and Buddhism. Christianity and Buddhism are primarily personal religions, with mystical doctrines and a love of contemplation.

===Chinese===

这种渴望导致了不愿放弃东方政策，尽管人们认识到，在放弃该政策之前，与资本主义英国实现和平是不可能的。我不知道是否有人会产生这样的念头：如果英国走向革命，我们就会愿意把印度拱手让给俄国人。但我确信相反的念头是存在的，即如果能够把印度从我们手中夺走，对帝国主义情绪的打击可能会引向我们的革命。无论在哪种情况下，西方革命与东方征服（伪装成解放受压迫民族）这两项政策都在协同运作，严丝合缝地融合成一个高度连贯的整体。

布尔什维主义作为一种社会现象，应当被视为一种宗教，而非普通的政治运动。对待世界的重要且有效的精神态度，大致可以划分为宗教的态度与科学的态度。科学的态度是试探性且逐步渐进的，相信其找到证据之物，且仅此而已。自伽利略（Galileo）以来，科学态度已被证明越来越能够查明重要的事实和定律，这些事实和定律得到了所有胜任之人的一致承认，而不论其性情、私利或政治压力如何。世界上自远古以来的几乎所有进步，都可以归功于科学和科学精神；而几乎所有的重大灾祸，都可以归咎于宗教。

我所说的宗教，是指一套作为教条被坚守、支配生活行为、超越或违背证据，并通过情感或权威而非智力方法进行灌输的信仰。根据这一定义，布尔什维主义就是一种宗教：它的教条超越或违背证据，我将在下文中竭力证明这一点。那些接受布尔什维主义的人变得对科学证据无动于衷，并不惜实施智力上的自杀。即便布尔什维主义的所有学说都是真实的，情况也依然如此，因为对它们的任何客观公正的审视都是不被容忍的。一个像我一样相信自由的心智是人类进步主要引擎的人，不能不对布尔什维主义抱有根本性的反对，正如对罗马教会（Church of Rome）一样。

在各种宗教中，布尔什维主义应当归入伊斯兰教一类，而非基督教和佛教。基督教和佛教主要是个人宗教，带有神秘主义教义和对沉思冥想的热爱。

===Original===

Mohammedanism and Bolshevism are practical, social, unspiritual, concerned to win the empire of this world. Their founders would not have resisted the third of the temptations in the wilderness. What Mohammedanism did for the Arabs, Bolshevism may do for the Russians. As Ali went down before the politicians who only rallied to the Prophet after his success, so the genuine Communists may go down before those who are now rallying to the ranks of the Bolsheviks. If so, Asiatic empire with all its pomps and splendours may well be the next stage of development, and Communism may seem, in historical retrospect, as small a part of Bolshevism as abstinence from alcohol is of Mohammedanism. It is true that, as a world force, whether for revolution or for empire, Bolshevism must sooner or later be brought by success into a desperate conflict with America; and America is more solid and strong, as yet, than anything that Mohammed’s followers had to face. But the doctrines of Communism are almost certain, in the long run, to make progress among American wage-earners, and the opposition of America is therefore not likely to be eternal. Bolshevism may go under in Russia, but even if it does it will spring up again elsewhere, since it is ideally suited to an industrial population in distress. What is evil in it is mainly due to the fact that it has its origin in distress; the problem is to disentangle the good from the evil, and induce the adoption of the good in countries not goaded into ferocity by despair.
Russia is a backward country, not yet ready for the methods of equal cooperation which the West is seeking to substitute for arbitrary power in politics and industry. In Russia, the methods of the Bolsheviks are probably more or less unavoidable; at any rate, I am not prepared to criticize them in their broad lines. But they are not the methods appropriate to more advanced countries, and our Socialists will be unnecessarily retrograde if they allow the prestige of the Bolsheviks to lead them into slavish imitation. It will be a far less excusable error in our reactionaries if, by their unteachableness, they compel the adoption of violent methods. We have a heritage of civilization and mutual tolerance which is important to ourselves and to the world.

===Chinese===

伊斯兰教和布尔什维主义是务实的、社会的、非精神层面的，专注于赢得尘世的帝国。两者的奠基者都不会抗拒旷野中的第三个诱惑。伊斯兰教为阿拉伯人所做的一切，布尔什维主义也可能为俄国人做到。正如阿里（Ali）败在了那些直到先知取得胜利后才聚集到其周围的政客手中一样，真正的共产党员也可能会败在那些如今正涌入布尔什维克行列的人手中。如果是这样，带有其全部奢华与荣耀的亚洲帝国很可能就是发展的下一阶段，而在历史回溯中，共产主义在布尔什维主义中所占的比重，可能就像戒酒在伊斯兰教中所占的比重一样微不足道。诚然，作为一种世界力量，无论是为了革命还是为了帝国，布尔什维主义迟早会由于其成功而与美国陷入一场绝望的冲突；而美国迄今为止比穆罕默德的追随者所面对的任何力量都要更加坚固和强大。但从长远来看，共产主义的学说几乎肯定会在美国工薪劳动者中取得进展，因此美国的反对不太可能是永恒的。布尔什维主义可能在俄国垮台，但即便如此它也会在其他地方重新兴起，因为它极其适合处于水深火热之中的工业人口。它其中邪恶的成分主要是由于它起源于困顿苦难这一事实；当前的问题是将善与恶区分开来，并促使那些未被绝望驱使走向残暴的国家采纳其善的部分。

俄国是一个落后的国家，尚未准备好采用西方正试图用以替代政治和工业中专断权力的平等合作方法。在俄国，布尔什维克的方法或许或多或少是不可避免的；无论如何，我不打算在宏观框架上对其提出批评。但它们并不是适用于更先进国家的方法，如果我们的社会主义者听任布尔什维克的声望引诱他们进行盲目盲从，那将是不必要的倒退。如果我们的反动派因其冥顽不化而迫使人们采用暴力手段，那将是一个更加不可饶恕的错误。我们拥有文明和相互宽容的遗产，这对于我们自身以及整个世界都是至关重要的。

===Original===

Life in Russia has always been fierce and cruel, to a far greater degree than with us, and out of the war has come a danger that this fierceness and cruelty may become universal. I have hopes that in England this may be avoided through the moderation of both sides. But it is essential to a happy issue that melodrama should no longer determine our views of the Bolsheviks: they are neither angels to be worshipped nor devils to be exterminated, but merely bold and able men attempting with great skill an almost impossible task.

===Chinese===

俄国的生活历来凶险残暴，其程度远甚于我们，而从战争中产生了一种危险，即这种凶险与残暴可能会走向普遍化。我抱有希望，在英国通过双方的克制可以避免这种情况。但要取得令人欣慰的结果，关键在于情节剧式的思维不应再决定我们对布尔什维克的看法：他们既不是受人顶礼膜拜的天使，也不是该被赶尽杀绝的魔鬼，而仅仅是一群大胆能干的人，正以极大的技巧尝试完成一项几乎不可能完成的任务。

# Part II — The Theory of Bolshevism / 第二部分 布尔什维主义的理论
===Original===



===Chinese===

第二部分 布尔什维主义的理论

## I The Materialistic Theory of History / I 唯物史观
===Original===

The materialistic conception of history, as it is called, is due to Marx, and underlies the whole Communist philosophy. I do not mean, of course, that a man could not be a Communist without accepting it, but that in fact it is accepted by the Communist Party, and that it profoundly influences their views as to politics and tactics. The name does not convey at all accurately what is meant by the theory. It means that all the mass-phenomena of history are determined by economic motives. This view has no essential connection with materialism in the philosophic sense. Materialism in the philosophic sense may be defined as the theory that all apparently mental occurrences either are really physical, or at any rate have purely physical causes. Materialism in this sense also was preached by Marx, and is accepted by all orthodox Marxians. The arguments for and against it are long and complicated, and need not concern us, since, in fact, its truth or falsehood has little or no bearing on politics.
In particular, philosophic materialism does not prove that economic causes are fundamental in politics. The view of Buckle, for example, according to which climate is one of the decisive factors, is equally compatible with materialism. So is the Freudian view, which traces everything to sex. There are innumerable ways of viewing history which are materialistic in the philosophic sense without being economic or falling within the Marxian formula. Thus the “materialistic conception of history” may be false even if materialism in the philosophic sense should be true.
On the other hand, economic causes might be at the bottom of all political events even if philosophic materialism were false. Economic causes operate through men’s desire for possessions, and would be supreme if this desire were supreme, even if desire could not, from a philosophic point of view, be explained in materialistic terms.
There is, therefore, no logical connection either way between philosophic materialism and what is called the “materialistic conception of history.”
It is of some moment to realize such facts as this, because otherwise political theories are both supported and opposed for quite irrelevant reasons, and arguments of theoretical philosophy are employed to determine questions which depend upon concrete facts of human nature.

===Chinese===

所谓的唯物史观（materialistic conception of history，即历史唯物主义），源于马克思（Marx），构成了整个共产主义（Communism）哲学的基础。我当然不是说一个人如果不接受唯物史观就不能成为共产党员，但事实上它为共产党所接受，并深刻影响了他们关于政治和策略的看法。这一名称根本无法准确传达该理论的本义。它的意思是：历史上的一切群体现象都是由经济动机决定的。这种观点与哲学意义上的唯物主义并无本质联系。哲学唯物主义（philosophic materialism）可以被定义为这样一种理论：所有表面上的精神现象要么实际上是物理现象，要么无论如何都纯粹具有物理原因。这种意义上的唯物主义也由马克思所宣扬，并为所有正统马克思主义者所接受。支持和反对它的论辩冗长而复杂，无需我们过多关注，因为事实上，它的真伪与政治几乎毫无干系。

尤其是，哲学唯物主义并不能证明经济原因在政治中是根本性的。例如，巴克尔（Buckle）认为气候是决定性因素之一的观点，同样与唯物主义相兼容。弗洛伊德将一切归因于性的观点亦是如此。在哲学意义上属于唯物主义、却并非经济决定论或不属于马克思主义公式的历史观是不计其数的。因此，即便哲学意义上的唯物主义是正确的，“唯物史观”也可能是错误的。

另一方面，即便哲学唯物主义是错误的，经济原因也可能是一切政治事件的根底所在。经济原因通过人们对财产的占有欲起作用，只要这种欲望是至高无上的，那么即便这种欲望从哲学角度无法用唯物主义术语来解释，经济原因也将处于支配地位。

因此，在哲学唯物主义与所谓的“唯物史观”之间，无论从哪个方向看，都不存在逻辑上的必然联系。

认识到诸如此类的事实具有一定的意义，因为否则的话，政治理论就会因完全无关的理由而受到支持或反对，而理论哲学的论证就会被用于裁定取决于人性具体事实的问题。

===Original===

This mixture damages both philosophy and politics, and is therefore important to avoid.
For another reason, also, the attempt to base a political theory upon a philosophical doctrine is undesirable. The philosophical doctrine of materialism, if true at all, is true everywhere and always; we cannot expect exceptions to it, say, in Buddhism or in the Hussite movement. And so it comes about that people whose politics are supposed to be a consequence of their metaphysics grow absolute and sweeping, unable to admit that a general theory of history is likely, at best, to be only true on the whole and in the main. The dogmatic character of Marxian Communism finds support in the supposed philosophic basis of the doctrine; it has the fixed certainty of Catholic theology, not the changing fluidity and sceptical practicality of modern science.
Treated as a practical approximation, not as an exact metaphysical law, the materialistic conception of history has a very large measure of truth. Take, as an instance of its truth, the influence of industrialism upon ideas. It is industrialism, rather than the arguments of Darwinians and Biblical critics, that has led to the decay of religious belief in the urban working class. At the same time, industrialism has revived religious belief among the rich. In the eighteenth century French aristocrats mostly became freethinkers; now their descendants are mostly Catholics, because it has become necessary for all the forces of reaction to unite against the revolutionary proletariat. Take, again, the emancipation of women. Plato, Mary Wolstonecraft, and John Stuart Mill produced admirable arguments, but influenced only a few impotent idealists. The war came, leading to the employment of women in industry on a large scale, and instantly the arguments in favour of votes for women were seen to be irresistible. More than that, traditional sexual morality collapsed, because its whole basis was the economic dependence of women upon their fathers and husbands. Changes in such a matter as sexual morality bring with them profound alterations in the thoughts and feelings of ordinary men and women; they modify law, literature, art, and all kinds of institutions that seem remote from economics.

===Chinese===

这种混淆对哲学和政治都有害无益，因此避免这种混淆是至关重要的。

此外，试图将政治理论建立在哲学学说之上的做法也是不可取的，这还有另一个原因。唯物主义的哲学学说如果为真，那就是在任何地方、任何时候都为真的；我们不能期望在例如佛教或胡斯派运动（Hussite movement）中存在特例。于是，那些政治立场被认为源于其形而上学的人，就会变得绝对化和以偏概全，无法承认一般的历史理论充其量只可能在整体上和大体上为真。马克思主义共产主义的教条主义特征，在其学说自诩的哲学基础中找到了支撑；它具有天主教神学的固定确定性，而非现代科学那种不断变化的流动性与怀疑求实的实用性。

如果将其视为实际上的近似法则，而非精确的形而上学定律，唯物史观包含着非常大程度的真理成分。以工业主义对思想观念的影响为例，即可见其真理性的一斑。正是工业主义，而非达尔文学派（Darwinians）的论证或《圣经》考据家的批评，导致了城市工人阶级宗教信仰的衰落。与此同时，工业主义却使富人阶层的宗教信仰得以复苏。在十八世纪，法国贵族大多成了自由思想者；而如今他们的后裔大多是天主教徒，因为一切反动力量必须联合起来对付革命无产阶级，这已成为必然。再以妇女解放为例。柏拉图（Plato）、玛丽·沃斯通克拉夫特（Mary Wollstonecraft）和约翰·斯图尔特·密尔（John Stuart Mill）提出了令人赞叹的论证，但仅仅影响了少数无能为力的理想主义者。战争爆发了，导致妇女被大规模雇用进工业领域，支持妇女选举权的论点瞬间变得无可抗拒。不仅如此，传统的性道德也分崩离析了，因为它的全部基础就在于妇女在经济上对其父亲和丈夫的依赖。像性道德这类事项的改变，给普通男女的思想和情感带来了深刻的变革；它们改变了法律、文学、艺术以及看起来与经济相去甚远的各类制度。

===Original===

Such facts as these justify Marxians in speaking, as they do, of “bourgeois ideology,” meaning that kind of morality which has been imposed upon the world by the possessors of capital. Contentment with one’s lot may be taken as typical of the virtues preached by the rich to the poor. They honestly believe it is a virtue﻿—at any rate they did formerly. The more religious among the poor also believed it, partly from the influence of authority, partly from an impulse to submission, what MacDougall calls “negative self-feeling,” which is commoner than some people think. Similarly men preached the virtue of female chastity, and women usually accepted their teaching; both really believed the doctrine, but its persistence was only possible through the economic power of men. This led erring women to punishment here on earth, which made further punishment hereafter seem probable. When the economic penalty ceased, the conviction of sinfulness gradually decayed. In such changes we see the collapse of “bourgeois ideology.”
But in spite of the fundamental importance of economic facts in determining the politics and beliefs of an age or nation, I do not think that non-economic factors can be neglected without risks of errors which may be fatal in practice.
The most obvious non-economic factor, and the one the neglect of which has led Socialists most astray, is nationalism. Of course a nation, once formed, has economic interests which largely determine its politics; but it is not, as a rule, economic motives that decide what group of human beings shall form a nation. Trieste, before the war, considered itself Italian, although its whole prosperity as a port depended upon its belonging to Austria. No economic motive can account for the opposition between Ulster and the rest of Ireland. In Eastern Europe, the Balkanization produced by self-determination has been obviously disastrous from an economic point of view, and was demanded for reasons which were in essence sentimental. Throughout the war wage-earners, with only a few exceptions, allowed themselves to be governed by nationalist feeling, and ignored the traditional Communist exhortation: “Workers of the world, unite.” According to Marxian orthodoxy, they were misled by cunning capitalists, who made their profit out of the slaughter.

===Chinese===

诸如此类的事实证明了马克思主义者谈论“资产阶级意识形态”（bourgeois ideology）是完全有理由的，它指的是那种由资本拥有者强加给世界的道德。安于天命可以被视为富人向穷人宣扬的典型美德。他们由衷地相信这是一种美德——无论如何他们以前是这么认为的。穷人中较为虔诚的人也相信这一点，部分是由于权威的影响，部分是出于顺从的冲动，即麦独孤（MacDougall）所谓的“消极自我感”（negative self-feeling），这比某些人想象的更为普遍。同样，男人们宣扬女性贞洁的美德，而妇女通常也接受了这种教导；双方都真正相信这一教条，但它的维系唯有通过男性的经济权力才有可能。这导致犯错的妇女在今世遭受惩罚，而这使得在来世遭受进一步惩罚显得合乎情理。当经济惩罚消失时，有罪感的信念便逐渐消逝。在这些变化中，我们看到了“资产阶级意识形态”的瓦解。

然而，尽管经济事实在决定一个时代或一个民族的政治与信仰方面具有根本的重要性，但我认为绝不能忽视非经济因素，否则可能导致在实践中致命的错误风险。

最显而易见的非经济因素，也是对它的忽视导致社会主义者误入歧途最深的因素，就是民族主义。当然，一个民族一旦形成，就拥有在很大程度上决定其政治的经济利益；但通常而言，决定哪一群人类应当构成一个民族的，并非经济动机。战前的里雅斯特（Trieste）自认为是意大利的一部分，尽管作为港口，其全部繁荣完全依赖于从属于奥地利。任何经济动机都无法解释乌尔斯特（Ulster）与爱尔兰其余部分之间的对立。在东欧，由民族自决（self-determination）所造成的巴尔干化（Balkanization），从经济角度来看显然是灾难性的，但人们要求这么做却是出于本质上属于情感的理由。在整个战争期间，工薪劳动者除极少数特例外，都听任自己受民族主义情绪支配，而置传统的共产主义劝诫“全世界无产者，联合起来”于不顾。根据马克思主义正统观念，他们是受到了从屠杀中牟利的狡猾资本家的误导。

===Original===

But to anyone capable of observing psychological facts, it is obvious that this is largely a myth. Immense numbers of capitalists were ruined by the war; those who were young were just as liable to be killed as the proletarians were. No doubt commercial rivalry between England and Germany had a great deal to do with causing the war; but rivalry is a different thing from profit-seeking. Probably by combination English and German capitalists could have made more than they did out of rivalry, but the rivalry was instinctive, and its economic form was accidental. The capitalists were in the grip of nationalist instinct as much as their proletarian “dupes.” In both classes some have gained by the war; but the universal will to war was not produced by the hope of gain. It was produced by a different set of instincts, and one which Marxian psychology fails to recognize adequately.
The Marxian assumes that a man’s “herd,” from the point of view of herd-instinct, is his class, and that he will combine with those whose economic class-interest is the same as his. This is only very partially true in fact. Religion has been the most decisive factor in determining a man’s herd throughout long periods of the world’s history. Even now a Catholic working man will vote for a Catholic capitalist rather than for an unbelieving Socialist. In America the divisions in local elections are mainly on religious lines. This is no doubt convenient for the capitalists, and tends to make them religious men; but the capitalists alone could not produce the result. The result is produced by the fact that many working men prefer the advancement of their creed to the improvement of their livelihood. However deplorable such a state of mind may be, it is not necessarily due to capitalist lies.
All politics are governed by human desires. The materialist theory of history, in the last analysis, requires the assumption that every politically conscious person is governed by one single desire﻿—the desire to increase his own share of commodities; and, further, that his method of achieving this desire will usually be to seek to increase the share of his class, not only his own individual share. But this assumption is very far from the truth. Men desire power, they desire satisfactions for their pride and their self-respect.

===Chinese===

但对任何有能力观察心理事实的人来说，显而易见这在很大程度上是一个神话。数不胜数的资本家因战争而破产倾家荡产；年轻的资本家与无产者一样容易阵亡。毫无疑问，英德之间的商业竞争在引发战争方面起到了很大作用；但竞争与牟利是两码事。英德资本家通过联合本可以比通过竞争赚取更多的利润，但竞争是出于本能的，而其经济形式则是偶然的。资本家同受其蒙骗的无产者一样，受制于民族主义本能的支配。在两个阶级中，都有人从战争中获利；但普遍走向战争的意志并非由牟利的希望所产生。它是由一套不同的本能所产生的，而马克思主义心理学未能充分认识到这套本能。

马克思主义者假定，从合群本能（herd-instinct，群居本能）的角度来看，一个人的“群”就是他的阶级，他会与那些经济阶级利益与他相同的人联合起来。事实上，这只是非常局部的真理。在人类历史的漫长时期中，宗教始终是决定一个人归属于哪个群体的最决定性因素。即便在今天，一个天主教工人宁愿投票支持天主教资本家，也不会投票支持不信教的社会主义者。在美国，地方选举中的分野主要是按宗教界限划分的。这无疑对资本家有利，并倾向于使他们成为宗教信徒；但仅凭资本家单方面的力量是无法产生这种结果的。这种结果的产生是由于这样一个事实：许多工人宁愿追求其教条信条的弘扬，也不愿改善自己的生计。无论这种心态多么令人叹惜，它并不一定归咎于资本家的谎言。

一切政治都受人类欲望的支配。归根结底，唯物史观要求预设这样一个假设：每一个具有政治意识的人都受单一欲望的支配——即增加自己那一份商品份额的欲望；而且，他实现这一欲望的方法通常是谋求增加其所属阶级的份额，而不仅是他个人的份额。但这一假设与事实相去甚远。人们渴望权力，他们渴望满足自己的虚荣心和自尊心。

===Original===

They desire victory over rivals so profoundly that they will invent a rivalry for the unconscious purpose of making a victory possible. All these motives cut across the pure economic motive in ways that are practically important.
There is need of a treatment of political motives by the methods of psychoanalysis. In politics, as in private life, men invent myths to rationalize their conduct. If a man thinks that the only reasonable motive in politics is economic self-advancement, he will persuade himself that the things he wishes to do will make him rich. When he wants to fight the Germans, he tells himself that their competition is ruining his trade. If, on the other hand, he is an “idealist,” who holds that his politics should aim at the advancement of the human race, he will tell himself that the crimes of the Germans demand their humiliation. The Marxian sees through this latter camouflage, but not through the former. To desire one’s own economic advancement is comparatively reasonable; to Marx, who inherited eighteenth-century rationalist psychology from the British orthodox economists, self-enrichment seemed the natural aim of a man’s political actions. But modern psychology has dived much deeper into the ocean of insanity upon which the little barque of human reason insecurely floats. The intellectual optimism of a bygone age is no longer possible to the modern student of human nature. Yet it lingers in Marxism, making Marxians rigid and Procrustean in their treatment of the life of instinct. Of this rigidity the materialistic conception of history is a prominent instance.
In the next chapter I shall attempt to outline a political psychology which seems to me more nearly true than that of Marx.

===Chinese===

他们对战胜对手的渴望是如此强烈，以至于会出于无意识的目的虚构出一场竞争，以使胜利成为可能。所有这些动机都以在实践中具有重要意义的方式切断了纯粹的经济动机。

有必要运用精神分析（psychoanalysis）的方法来审视政治动机。在政治中正如在个人生活中一样，人们编造神话来使自己的行为合理化。如果一个人认为政治中唯一合理的动机是经济上的自我提升，他就会说服自己相信自己想做的事情会让自己致富。当他想要同德国人作战时，他告诉自己德国人的竞争正在摧毁他的生意。如果另一方面，他是一个认为自己的政治应当致力于全人类进步的“理想主义者”，他就会告诉自己德国人的罪行要求对其予以惩罚羞辱。马克思主义者能够识破后一种伪装，却识不破前一种。渴望自身的经济提升是相对合理的；对于从英国正统经济学家那里继承了十八世纪理性主义心理学的马克思而言，自我致富似乎是一个人政治行动的自然目的。然而现代心理学已经深入探究到了疯狂的海洋深处，而人类理性的扁舟不过危如累卵般漂浮在这片海洋之上。对于现代的人性研究者而言，逝去时代的智力乐观主义已不再可能。然而它却在马克思主义中阴魂不散，使得马克思主义者在对待本能生活时表现得僵化生硬且具有普罗克鲁斯忒斯式的强求一致（Procrustean）。在唯物史观中，这种僵化表现得尤为突出。

在下一章中，我将尝试勾勒出一种在我看来比马克思的学说更接近真理的政治心理学。

## II Deciding Forces in Politics / II 政治中的决定性力量
===Original===

The larger events in the political life of the world are determined by the interaction of material conditions and human passions. The operation of the passions on the material conditions is modified by intelligence. The passions themselves may be modified by alien intelligence guided by alien passions. So far, such modification has been wholly unscientific, but it may in time become as precise as engineering.
The classification of the passions which is most convenient in political theory is somewhat different from that which would be adopted in psychology.
We may begin with desires for the necessaries of life: food, drink, sex, and (in cold climates) clothing and housing. When these are threatened, there is no limit to the activity and violence that men will display.
Planted upon these primitive desires are a number of secondary desires. Love of property, of which the fundamental political importance is obvious, may be derived historically and psychologically from the hoarding instinct. Love of the good opinion of others (which we may call vanity) is a desire which man shares with many animals; it is perhaps derivable from courtship, but has great survival value, among gregarious animals, in regard to others besides possible mates. Rivalry and love of power are perhaps developments of jealousy; they are akin, but not identical.
These four passions﻿—acquisitiveness, vanity, rivalry, and love of power﻿—are, after the basic instincts, the prime movers of almost all that happens in politics. Their operation is intensified and regularized by herd instinct. But herd instinct, by its very nature, cannot be a prime mover, since it merely causes the herd to act in unison, without determining what the united action is to be. Among men, as among other gregarious animals, the united action, in any given circumstances, is determined partly by the common passions of the herd, partly by imitation of leaders. The art of politics consists in causing the latter to prevail over the former.
Of the four passions we have enumerated, only one, namely acquisitiveness, is concerned at all directly with men’s relations to their material conditions. The other three﻿—vanity, rivalry, and love of power﻿—are concerned with social relations.

===Chinese===

世界政治生活中的重大事件，是由物质条件与人类激情的相互作用所决定的。激情对物质条件的作用受到智力的调节。激情本身也可能受到由外在激情引导的外在智力的调节。迄今为止，这种调节完全是非科学的，但假以时日，它可能会变得像工程学一样精确。

在政治理论中最便于使用的激情分类，与心理学中所采用的分类略有不同。

我们可以从对生活必需品的欲望开始：食物、饮水、性，以及（在寒冷气候下的）衣物与住房。当这些受到威胁时，人类所展现的活动与暴力将是毫无节制的。

植根于这些原始欲望之上的，是许多次生欲望。占有财产的欲望——其在政治上的根本重要性是不言而喻的——在历史和心理上可以追溯到储藏本能。渴望获得他人的好评（我们称之为虚荣），是人类与许多动物共有的一种欲望；它或许衍生自求偶行为，但在群居动物中，对于潜在配偶之外的其他个体而言，它具有巨大的生存价值。竞争与对权力的热爱，或许是嫉妒的延伸；它们彼此相似，但并不相同。

这四种激情——占有欲、虚荣心、竞争心与对权力的热爱——仅次于基本本能，构成了政治中发生之几乎一切事件的原动力。它们的作用通过合群本能（群居本能）得到了强化与规则化。但合群本能就其本质而言，不可能是原动力，因为它仅仅促使群体步调一致地行动，却不决定联合行动究竟是什么。在人类中间，正如在其他群居动物中间一样，在任何给定的环境下，联合行动部分取决于群体的共同激情，部分取决于对领袖的模仿。政治的艺术就在于使后者压倒前者。

在我们列举的四种激情中，只有一种，即占有欲，与人类同其物质条件的关系有着最为直接的联系。其余三种——虚荣心、竞争心与对权力的热爱——则与社会关系密切相关。

===Original===

I think this is the source of what is erroneous in the Marxian interpretation of history, which tacitly assumes that acquisitiveness is the source of all political actions. It is clear that many men willingly forego wealth for the sake of power and glory, and that nations habitually sacrifice riches to rivalry with other nations. The desire for some form of superiority is common to almost all energetic men. No social system which attempts to thwart it can be stable, since the lazy majority will never be a match for the energetic minority.
What is called “virtue” is an offshoot of vanity: it is the habit of acting in a manner which others praise.
The operation of material conditions may be illustrated by the statement (Myers’s *Dawn of History*) that four of the greatest movements of conquest have been due to drought in Arabia, causing the nomads of that country to migrate into regions already inhabited. The last of these four movements was the rise of Islam. In these four cases, the primal need of food and drink was enough to set events in motion; but as this need could only be satisfied by conquest, the four secondary passions must have very soon come into play. In the conquests of modern industrialism, the secondary passions have been almost wholly dominant, since those who directed them had no need to fear hunger or thirst. It is the potency of vanity and love of power that gives hope for the industrial future of Soviet Russia, since it enables the Communist State to enlist in its service men whose abilities might give them vast wealth in a capitalistic society.
Intelligence modifies profoundly the operation of material conditions. When America was first discovered, men only desired gold and silver; consequently the portions first settled were not those that are now most profitable. The Bessemer process created the German iron and steel industry; inventions requiring oil have created a demand for that commodity which is one of the chief influences in international politics.
The intelligence which has this profound effect on politics is not political, but scientific and technical: it is the kind of intelligence which discovers how to make nature minister to human passions. Tungsten had no value until it was found to be useful in the manufacture of shells and electric light, but now people will, if necessary, kill each other in order to acquire tungsten.

===Chinese===

我认为这就是马克思主义历史解释中错误成分的根源所在，该解释默认占有欲是一切政治行动的源头。显而易见，许多人为了权力和荣耀而甘愿放弃财富，而且各民族习惯于为了同其他民族竞争而牺牲财富。对某种形式优越地位的渴望，几乎为所有精力充沛的人所共有。任何试图阻挠这种渴望的社会制度都不可能稳固，因为怠惰的多数人永远不是精力充沛的少数人的对手。

所谓的“美德”是虚荣心的产物：它是以他人赞许的方式行事的习惯。

物质条件的作用可以通过这样一种论述来说明（迈尔斯《历史的黎明》[Myers’s *Dawn of History*]）：四次最重大的征服运动都是由于阿拉伯半岛的干旱引起的，干旱迫使该国的游牧民迁徙到已经有人居住的地区。这四次运动中的最后一次便是伊斯兰教的兴起。在这四种情况下，对食物和饮水的原始需求足以推动事态的发展；但由于这种需求只有通过征服才能得到满足，四种次生激情必然很快发挥了作用。在现代工业主义的征服中，次生激情几乎完全占据了主导地位，因为指导征服的人无需畏惧饥饿或干渴。正是虚荣心与对权力热爱的巨大威力，为苏维埃俄国工业的未来带来了希望，因为它使共产主义国家能够让那些在资本主义社会中可能凭借其才能获取巨额财富的人为其效力。

智力深刻地调节着物质条件的作用。当美洲最初被发现时，人们仅仅渴望黄金和白银；因此最初定居的地区并非如今最有利可图的地区。贝塞麦炼钢法（Bessemer process）创造了德国的钢铁工业；需要石油的发明创造了对该商品的需求，而这一需求构成了国际政治中的主要影响因素之一。

对政治产生这种深远影响的智力并不是政治智力，而是科学与技术智力：它是一种发现如何使自然服务于人类激情的智力。钨过去毫无价值，直到人们发现它可用于制造炮弹和电灯，但如今如果有必要，人们会为了夺取钨而自相残杀。科学智力就是导致这种改变的原因。

===Original===

Scientific intelligence is the cause of this change.
The progress or retrogression of the world depends, broadly speaking, upon the balance between acquisitiveness and rivalry. The former makes for progress, the latter for retrogression. When intelligence provides improved methods of production, these may be employed to increase the general share of goods, or to set apart more of the labour power of the community for the business of killing its rivals. Until 1914, acquisitiveness had prevailed, on the whole, since the fall of Napoleon; the past six years have seen a prevalence of the instinct of rivalry. Scientific intelligence makes it possible to indulge this instinct more fully than is possible for primitive peoples, since it sets free more men from the labour of producing necessaries. It is possible that scientific intelligence may, in time, reach the point when it will enable rivalry to exterminate the human race. This is the most hopeful method of bringing about an end of war.
For those who do not like this method, there is another: the study of scientific psychology and physiology. The physiological causes of emotions have begun to be known, through the studies of such men as Cannon (*Bodily Changes in Pain, Hunger, Fear and Rage*). In time, it may become possible, by physiological means, to alter the whole emotional nature of a population. It will then depend upon the passions of the rulers how this power is used. Success will come to the State which discovers how to promote pugnacity to the extent required for external war, but not to the extent which would lead to domestic dissensions. There is no method by which it can be insured that rulers shall desire the good of mankind, and therefore there is no reason to suppose that the power to modify men’s emotional nature would cause progress.
If men desired to diminish rivalry, there is an obvious method. Habits of power intensify the passion of rivalry; therefore a State in which power is concentrated will, other things being equal, be more bellicose than one in which power is diffused. For those who dislike wars, this is an additional argument against all forms of dictatorship. But dislike of war is far less common than we used to suppose; and those who like war can use the same argument to support dictatorship.

===Chinese===

广义而言，世界的进步或倒退取决于占有欲与竞争心之间的平衡。前者促进进步，后者导致倒退。当智力提供了改良的生产方法时，这些方法可以被用来增加商品的普遍份额，也可以被用来把社会更多的劳动力抽调出来用于杀戮对手的事业。从拿破仑（Napoleon）垮台到 1914 年，总的来说占有欲一直占据主导地位；而过去的六年则目睹了竞争本能的泛滥。科学智力使得人们能够比原始民族更加充分地纵容这种本能，因为它将更多的人从生产生活必需品的劳作中解放出来。假以时日，科学智力有可能达到这样一个临界点，即使得竞争能够彻底灭绝人类。这是带来战争终结的最充满希望的方法。

对于那些不喜欢这种方法的人而言，还有另一种途径：对科学心理学和生理学的研究。通过诸如坎农（Cannon，《疼痛、饥饿、恐惧和狂怒中的身体变化》[*Bodily Changes in Pain, Hunger, Fear and Rage*]）等人的研究，情绪的生理原因已开始为人们所知晓。假以时日，通过生理手段改变一个族群的整个情绪本质或许会成为可能。届时，如何使用这种力量将取决于统治者的激情。成功将降临到能够发现如何激起对外战争所需程度的好战性、同时又不至于引发国内内讧的国家身上。没有任何方法能够确保统治者必然渴望人类的福祉，因此没有任何理由假设改变人类情绪本质的力量必然会带来进步。

如果人们渴望减少竞争，有一个显而易见的方法。权力的习惯会加剧竞争的激情；因此，在其他条件相同的情况下，权力集中的国家将比权力分散的国家更加好战。对于那些厌恶战争的人而言，这是反对一切形式专政的又一论据。但对战争的厌恶远不如我们过去所设想的那么普遍；而那些喜欢战争的人则可以用同样的论据来支持专政。

## III Bolshevik Criticism of Democracy / III 布尔什维克对民主的批判
===Original===

The Bolshevik argument against Parliamentary democracy as a method of achieving Socialism is a powerful one. My answer to it lies rather in pointing out what I believe to be fallacies in the Bolshevik method, from which I conclude that no swift method exists of establishing any desirable form of Socialism. But let us first see what the Bolshevik argument is.
In the first place, it assumes that those to whom it is addressed are absolutely certain that Communism is desirable, so certain that they are willing, if necessary, to force it upon an unwilling population at the point of the bayonet. It then proceeds to argue that, while capitalism retains its hold over propaganda and its means of corruption, Parliamentary methods are very unlikely to give a majority for Communism in the House of Commons, or to lead to effective action by such a majority even if it existed. Communists point out how the people are deceived, and how their chosen leaders have again and again betrayed them. From this they argue that the destruction of capitalism must be sudden and catastrophic; that it must be the work of a minority; and that it cannot be effected constitutionally or without violence. It is therefore, in their view, the duty of the Communist party in a capitalist country to prepare for armed conflict, and to take all possible measure for disarming the bourgeoisie and arming that part of the proletariat which is willing to support the Communists.
There is an air of realism and disillusionment about this position, which makes it attractive to those idealists who wish to think themselves cynics. But I think there are various points in which it fails to be as realistic as it pretends.
In the first place, it makes much of the treachery of Labour leaders in constitutional movements, but does not consider the possibility of the treachery of Communist leaders in a revolution. To this the Marxian would reply that in constitutional movements men are bought, directly or indirectly, by the money of the capitalists, but that revolutionary Communism would leave the capitalists no money with which to attempt corruption. This has been achieved in Russia, and could be achieved elsewhere. But selling oneself to the capitalists is not the only possible form of treachery. It is also possible, having acquired power, to use it for one’s own ends instead of for the people.

===Chinese===

布尔什维克反对将议会民主制作为实现社会主义之手段的论点是强有力的。我对这一论点的回答，主要在于指出我认为在布尔什维克方法中所存在的谬误，并由此得出结论：不存在任何能够迅速建立起任何令人向往的社会主义形态的捷径。但让我们首先看看布尔什维克的论点究竟是什么。

首先，它假定其听众绝对确信共产主义（Communism）是值得追求的，确信到必要时不惜用刺刀将其强加给不情愿的民众。接着它论证道：只要资本主义依然保持着对宣传的控制及其收买腐蚀手段，议会途径就极不可能在下议院（平民院，House of Commons）为共产主义赢得多数席位；即便存在这样的多数，也极不可能带来有效的行动。共产党人指出人民是如何受蒙蔽的，以及他们选出的领袖是如何一次又一次背叛他们的。由此他们论证道：摧毁资本主义必须是突发性且灾难性的；它必须是少数人的作为；而且它不可能通过宪政方式或在没有暴力的情况下达成。因此，在他们看来，资本主义国家的共产党的职责就是准备武装冲突，并采取一切可能措施解除资产阶级的武装，同时武装愿意支持共产党员的那部分无产阶级。

这种立场带有一种现实主义与去幻想化的意味，使其对那些自命愤世嫉俗的理想主义者极具吸引力。但我认为，在很多方面它并不像其所标榜的那样具有现实主义色彩。

首先，它对宪政运动中工党领袖的背叛大做文章，却不考虑革命中共产党领袖背叛的可能性。对此，马克思主义者会反驳说，在宪政运动中，人们直接或间接地被资本家的金钱所收买，但革命共产主义将使资本家没有任何金钱可用于企图收买。这在俄国已经实现，在其他地方也可以实现。然而，向资本家出卖自己并不是背叛的唯一可能形式。在攫取权力之后，将权力用于谋取私利而非造福人民，也同样是可能的。

===Original===

This is what I believe to be likely to happen in Russia: the establishment of a bureaucratic aristocracy, concentrating authority in its own hands, and creating a regime just as oppressive and cruel as that of capitalism. Marxians never sufficiently recognize that love of power is quite as strong a motive, and quite as great a source of injustice, as love of money; yet this must be obvious to any unbiased student of politics. It is also obvious that the method of violent revolution leading to a minority dictatorship is one peculiarly calculated to create habits of despotism which would survive the crisis by which they were generated. Communist politicians are likely to become just like the politicians of other parties: a few will be honest, but the great majority will merely cultivate the art of telling a plausible tale with a view to tricking the people into entrusting them with power. The only possible way by which politicians as a class can be improved is the political and psychological education of the people, so that they may learn to detect a humbug. In England men have reached the point of suspecting a good speaker, but if a man speaks badly they think he must be honest. Unfortunately, virtue is not so widely diffused as this theory would imply.
In the second place, it is assumed by the Communist argument that, although capitalist propaganda can prevent the majority from becoming Communists, yet capitalist laws and police forces cannot prevent the Communists, while still a minority, from acquiring a supremacy of military power. It is thought that secret propaganda can undermine the army and navy, although it is admittedly impossible to get the majority to vote at elections for the programme of the Bolsheviks. This view is based upon Russian experience, where the army and navy had suffered defeat and had been brutally ill used by incompetent Tsarist authorities. The argument has no application to more efficient and successful States. Among the Germans, even in defeat, it was the civilian population that began the revolution.
There is a further assumption in the Bolshevik argument which seems to me quite unwarrantable. It is assumed that the capitalist governments will have learned nothing from the experience of Russia. Before the Russian Revolution, governments had not studied Bolshevik theory.

===Chinese===

这正是我认为很可能在俄国发生的事情：建立起一个官僚特权阶层，将权力集中于自己手中，并创造出一个与资本主义政权一样压迫和残酷的政权。马克思主义者从未充分认识到，对权力的热爱正如对金钱的热爱一样，是一种同样强烈的动机，也是同样巨大的不公正根源；然而对任何客观公正的政治研究者来说，这必然是不言自明的。同样显而易见的是，通过暴力革命导致少数人专政的方法，是一种极其容易养成专制习惯的方法，而这种习惯将在产生它们的危机过去之后依然长期存在。共产党政客很可能会变得与其他党派的政客别无二致：少数人是诚实的，但绝大多数人将仅仅专注于巧言令色，以诱使人民将权力托付给他们。改善政客阶层的唯一可行途径是对人民进行政治与心理教育，使他们能够识破骗局。在英国，人们已经达到了对善辩者产生怀疑的地步，但如果一个人拙于言辞，他们就认为他必然是诚实的。遗憾的是，美德的普及程度并不像这一理论所暗示的那么广泛。

其次，共产党人的论点假定，尽管资本主义的宣传能够阻止多数人成为共产党员，但资本主义的法律和警察力量却无法阻止仍然处于少数地位的共产党人获得军事力量上的优势。人们认为秘密宣传能够瓦解陆军和海军，尽管人们公认无法让多数人在选举中投票支持布尔什维克的纲领。这种观点是基于俄国的经验，在俄国，陆海军遭遇了战败，并受到了无能沙皇当局的残酷虐待。这一论点并不适用于更具效率且更成功的国家。

===Original===

And defeat in war created a revolutionary mood throughout Central and Eastern Europe. But now the holders of power are on their guard. There seems no reason whatever to suppose that they will supinely permit a preponderance of armed force to pass into the hands of those who wish to overthrow them, while, according to the Bolshevik theory, they are still sufficiently popular to be supported by a majority at the polls. Is it not as clear as noonday that in a democratic country it is more difficult for the proletariat to destroy the Government by arms than to defeat it in a general election? Seeing the immense advantages of a Government in dealing with rebels, it seems clear that rebellion could have little hope of success unless a very large majority supported it. Of course, if the army and navy were specially revolutionary, they might effect an unpopular revolution; but this situation, though something like it occurred in Russia, is hardly to be expected in the Western nations. This whole Bolshevik theory of revolution by a minority is one which might just conceivably have succeeded as a secret plot, but becomes impossible as soon as it is openly avowed and advocated.
But perhaps it will be said that I am caricaturing the Bolshevik doctrine of revolution. It is urged by advocates of this doctrine, quite truly, that all political events are brought about by minorities, since the majority are indifferent to politics. But there is a difference between a minority in which the indifferent acquiesce, and a minority so hated as to startle the indifferent into belated action. To make the Bolshevik doctrine reasonable, it is necessary to suppose that they believe the majority can be induced to acquiesce, at least temporarily, in the revolution made by the class-conscious minority. This, again, is based upon Russian experience: desire for peace and land led to a widespread support of the Bolsheviks in November 1917 on the part of people who have subsequently shown no love for Communism.
I think we come here to an essential part of Bolshevik philosophy. In the moment of revolution, Communists are to have some popular cry by which they win more support than mere Communism could win. Having thus acquired the State machine, they are to use it for their own ends. But this, again, is a method which can only be practised successfully so long as it is not avowed.

===Chinese===

而且在战争中的失败在整个中欧和东欧造成了革命的情绪。但如今当权者已经提高了警惕。没有任何理由假设他们会听任武装力量的优势落入那些意图推翻他们的人手中，而根据布尔什维克理论，他们此时在选票上依然广受欢迎并拥有多数人的支持。难道这不是如白昼般清晰的道理吗：在一个民主国家，无产阶级通过武力推翻政府，远比在大选中击败政府更加困难？鉴于政府在对付叛乱分子方面拥有的巨大优势，显而易见，除非有极其广大之多数人的支持，否则叛乱几乎毫无成功的希望。当然，如果陆军和海军特别具有革命性，他们可能会促成一场不受欢迎的革命；但这种局面——尽管在俄国曾发生过类似情况——在西方国家是难以指望的。布尔什维克这整套由少数人发动革命的理论，作为一种秘密阴谋或许勉强可以想象成功，但一旦被公开宣告和倡导，就变得彻底不可能了。

但也许有人会说我是在歪曲布尔什维克的革命学说。这一学说的拥护者相当真实地指出，一切政治事件都是由少数人促成的，因为多数人对政治漠不关心。但是在为漠不关心者所默认的少数，与那种令人憎恶以至于惊醒漠不关心者仓促行动的少数之间，存在着本质区别。要使布尔什维克的学说显得合理，就必须假定他们相信，能够诱导多数人至少暂时默认由有阶级意识的（class-conscious）少数人所发动的革命。这同样是基于俄国的经验：对和平与土地的渴望，导致了 1917 年 11 月广大民众对布尔什维克的广泛支持，而这些人随后并未表现出对共产主义的热爱。

我认为我们在此触及了布尔什维克哲学的核心部分。在革命关头，共产党人必须提出某种顺应民意的口号，以此赢得单凭纯粹共产主义所无法赢得的更多支持。在以此方式攫取国家机器之后，他们便将其用于自身的目的。但同样，这是一种唯有在其不公开言明的前提下才能成功施展的方法。

===Original===

It is to some extent habitual in politics. The Unionists in 1900 won a majority on the Boer War, and used it to endow brewers and Church schools. The Liberals in 1906 won a majority on Chinese labour, and used it to cement the secret alliance with France and to make an alliance with Tsarist Russia. President Wilson, in 1916, won his majority on neutrality, and used it to come into the war. This method is part of the stock-in-trade of democracy. But its success depends upon repudiating it until the moment comes to practise it. Those who, like the Bolsheviks, have the honesty to proclaim in advance their intention of using power for other ends than those for which it was given them, are not likely to have a chance of carrying out their designs.
What seems to me to emerge from these considerations is this: That in a democratic and politically educated country, armed revolution in favour of Communism would have no chance of succeeding unless it were supported by a larger majority than would be required for the election of a Communist Government by constitutional methods. It is possible that, if such a Government came into existence, and proceeded to carry out its programme, it would be met by armed resistance on the part of capital, including a large proportion of the officers in the army and navy. But in subduing this resistance it would have the support of that great body of opinion which believes in legality and upholds the constitution. Moreover, having, by hypothesis, converted a majority of the nation, a Communist Government could be sure of loyal help from immense numbers of workers, and would not be forced, as the Bolsheviks are in Russia, to suspect treachery everywhere. Under these circumstances, I believe that the resistance of the capitalists could be quelled without much difficulty, and would receive little support from moderate people. Whereas, in a minority revolt of Communists against a capitalist Government, all moderate opinion would be on the side of capitalism.
The contention that capitalist propaganda is what prevents the adoption of Communism by wage-earners is only very partially true. Capitalist propaganda has never been able to prevent the Irish from voting against the English, though it has been applied to this object with great vigour.

===Chinese===

在某种程度上，这在政治中是屡见不鲜的。统一党人在 1900 年凭借布尔什战争（Boer War）赢得了多数席位，并用其资助酿酒商和教会学校。自由党人在 1906 年凭借华工问题（Chinese labour）赢得了多数，并用其巩固了同法国的秘密结盟以及同沙皇俄国的结盟。威尔逊总统（President Wilson）在 1916 年凭借中立立场赢得了多数，并用其参与了战争。这种方法是民主政治现成手段的一部分。但它的成功取决于在付诸实践的时机成熟之前矢口否认。像布尔什维克那样坦诚地提前宣告他们打算将权力用于授予他们权力的目的之外的人，是不太可能有机会实现其企图的。

从这些考量中，在我看来所浮现出的结论是：在一个民主且受过政治教育的国家，支持共产主义的武装革命要想取得成功，所需要的多数必须比通过宪政方式选举共产主义政府所需的多数更为庞大，否则绝无成功的机会。如果这样一个政府诞生并着手执行其纲领，它可能会遭到资本势力的武装抵抗，包括陆海军中相当大一部分军官的抵抗，这是完全可能的。但在平定这种抵抗时，它将获得信仰合法性并捍卫宪法的广大民意群体的支持。此外，在假定已经争取到全国多数人支持的情况下，共产主义政府可以确信能够获得广大工人的忠诚协助，而不会像俄国的布尔什维克那样被迫处处怀疑叛徒。在这些情况下，我相信资本家的抵抗可以毫不费力地被扑灭，并且几乎得不到温和人士的支持。反之，在共产党员针对资本主义政府的少数人叛乱中，所有温和派的舆论都将站在资本主义一边。

所谓资本主义宣传阻碍了工薪劳动者接受共产主义的说法，只是非常局部的真理。资本主义宣传从未能阻止爱尔兰人投票反对英国人，尽管它曾极其卖力地运用于这一目标。

===Original===

It has proved itself powerless, over and over again, in opposing nationalist movements which had almost no moneyed support. It has been unable to cope with religious feeling. And those industrial populations which would most obviously benefit by Socialism have, in the main, adopted it, in spite of the opposition of employers. The plain truth is that Socialism does not arouse the same passionate interest in the average citizen as is roused by nationality and used to be roused by religion. It is not unlikely that things may change in this respect: we may be approaching a period of economic civil wars comparable to that of the religious civil wars that followed the Reformation. In such a period, nationalism is submerged by party: British and German Socialists, or British and German capitalists, will feel more kinship with each other than with compatriots of the opposite political camp. But when that day comes, there will be no difficulty, in highly industrial countries, in securing Socialist majorities; if Socialism is not then carried without bloodshed, it will be due to the unconstitutional action of the rich, not to the need of revolutionary violence on the part of the advocates of the proletariat. Whether such a state of opinion grows up or not depends mainly upon the stubbornness or conciliatoriness of the possessing classes, and, conversely, upon the moderation or violence of those who desire fundamental economic change. The majority which Bolsheviks regard as unattainable is chiefly prevented by the ruthlessness of their own tactics.
Apart from all arguments of detail, there are two broad objections to violent revolution in a democratic community. The first is that, when once the principle of respecting majorities as expressed at the ballot-box is abandoned, there is no reason to suppose that victory will be secured by the particular minority to which one happens to belong. There are many minorities besides Communists: religious minorities, teetotal minorities, militarist minorities, capitalist minorities. Any one of these could adopt the method of obtaining power advocated by the Bolsheviks, and any one would be just as likely to succeed as they are. What restrains these minorities, more or less, at present, is respect for the law and the constitution.

===Chinese===

事实一次又一次地证明，面对几乎没有任何金钱支持的民族主义运动，这种宣传是软弱无力的。它无法抗衡宗教情感。而那些从社会主义中获益最明显的工业人口，尽管受到雇主的反对，大体上依然采纳了社会主义。显而易见的实情是，社会主义并未激起普通公民像面对民族问题所激起的、以及过去面对宗教问题所曾激起的同样热烈的激情。在这方面情况并非不可能发生改变：我们可能正在走向一个经济国内战争的时期，可与宗教改革（Reformation）之后的宗教国内战争相提并论。在这样一个时期，民族主义被党派所淹没：英国与德国的社会主义者，或者英国与德国的资本家，彼此感受到的亲缘关系将超过他们同相反政治阵营的同胞之间的关系。但当那一天到来时，在高度工业化的国家确保社会主义多数席位将毫无困难；如果到那时不能在不流血的情况下推行社会主义，那将归因于富人的违宪行动，而非由于无产阶级拥护者需要采取革命暴力。这样一种舆论状态是否会形成，主要取决于占有阶级的顽固或和解态度，反过来也取决于渴望根本经济变革之人的温和或暴力程度。布尔什维克视为无法达成的多数，主要是被他们自己策略的冷酷无情所阻碍的。

抛开所有细节论点不谈，在民主社会中反对暴力革命有两个宏观理由。首先，一旦放弃尊重选票箱所体现之多数原则，就没有理由假设胜利一定会属于某人碰巧归属的那个特定少数派。除了共产党员之外，还有许许多多的少数派：宗教少数派、禁酒少数派、军国主义少数派、资本家少数派。其中任何一个都可以采纳布尔什维克所倡导的夺权方法，且任何一个都有与他们同样成功的可能。目前或多或少制约这些少数派的，是对法律和宪法的尊重。

===Original===

Bolsheviks tacitly assume that every other party will preserve this respect while they themselves, unhindered, prepare the revolution. But if their philosophy of violence becomes popular, there is not the slightest reason to suppose that they will be its beneficiaries. They believe that Communism is for the good of the majority; they ought to believe that they can persuade the majority on this question, and to have the patience to set about the task of winning by propaganda.
The second argument of principle against the method of minority violence is that abandonment of law, when it becomes widespread, lets loose the wild beast, and gives a free rein to the primitive lusts and egoisms which civilization in some degree curbs. Every student of medieval thought must have been struck by the extraordinarily high value placed upon law in that period. The reason was that, in countries infested by robber barons, law was the first requisite of progress. We, in the modern world, take it for granted that most people will be law-abiding, and we hardly realize what centuries of effort have gone to making such an assumption possible. We forget how many of the good things that we unquestionably expect would disappear out of life if murder, rape, and robbery with violence became common. And we forget even more how very easily this might happen. The universal class-war foreshadowed by the Third International, following upon the loosening of restraints produced by the late war, and combined with a deliberate inculcation of disrespect for law and constitutional government, might, and I believe would, produce a state of affairs in which it would be habitual to murder men for a crust of bread, and in which women would only be safe while armed men protected them. The civilized nations have accepted democratic government as a method of settling internal disputes without violence. Democratic government may have all the faults attributed to it, but it has the one great merit that people are, on the whole, willing to accept it as a substitute for civil war in political disputes. Whoever sets to work to weaken this acceptance, whether in Ulster or in Moscow, is taking a fearful responsibility. Civilization is not so stable that it cannot be broken up; and a condition of lawless violence is not one out of which any good thing is likely to emerge.

===Chinese===

布尔什维克默认所有其他党派都将保持这种尊重，而他们自己则毫无阻碍地准备革命。但如果他们的暴力哲学流行开来，没有任何丝毫理由假设他们会是其受益者。他们相信共产主义是为了多数人的利益；他们理应相信自己能够在这个问题上说服多数人，并有耐心着手进行通过宣传赢得多数的任务。

反对少数人暴力方法的第二个原则性论据是：法律的废弃一旦走向普遍，就会释放野兽，让文明在某种程度上予以遏制的原始欲望与利己主义肆意横行。每一位研究中世纪思想的学者，必然都会被那个时期对法律赋予的极高价值所震撼。原因在于，在盗匪贵族横行的国家，法律是进步的首要前提。我们在现代世界把大多数人遵纪守法视为理所当然，几乎没有意识到为了使这种假设成为可能付出了多少个世纪的努力。我们忘记了：如果谋杀、强奸和暴力抢劫成为司空见惯之事，我们在生活中毫无疑问所期盼的许多美好事物都将荡然无存。而我们更忘记了这种情况是何等容易发生。第三国际所预示的普遍阶级战争，紧随着最近这场战争所产生的约束松弛而来，再伴随着对法律和宪政政府不尊重的蓄意灌输，可能——且我相信必定——会产生这样一种局面：为了争夺一块面包皮而杀人成为常态，而妇女唯有在武装人员的保护下才能获得安全。文明国家已经接受了民主政府作为不诉诸暴力解决内部争端的方法。民主政府或许存在被归咎于它的所有缺点，但它拥有一个巨大的优点，那就是人们总体上愿意接受它作为政治争端中内战的替代物。无论是在乌尔斯特（Ulster）还是在莫斯科（Moscow），任何人只要着手削弱这种接受度，就背负着可怕的责任。文明并非稳固到不可瓦解；而一种无法无天的暴力状态，是绝不可能从中产生任何美好事物的。

===Original===

For this reason, if for no other, revolutionary violence in a democracy is infinitely dangerous.

===Chinese===

即便没有其他原因，单凭这一条，在民主体制下推行革命暴力也是极其危险的。

## IV Revolution and Dictatorship / IV 革命与专政
===Original===

The Bolsheviks have a very definite programme for achieving Communism﻿—a programme which has been set forth by Lenin repeatedly, and quite recently in the reply of the Third International to the questionnaire submitted by the Independent Labour Party.
Capitalists, we are assured, will stick at nothing in defence of their privileges. It is the nature of man, in so far as he is politically conscious, to fight for the interests of his class so long as classes exist. When the conflict is not pushed to extremes, methods of conciliation and political deception may be preferable to actual physical warfare; but as soon as the proletariat make a really vital attack upon the capitalists, they will be met by guns and bayonets. This being certain and inevitable, it is as well to be prepared for it, and to conduct propaganda accordingly. Those who pretend that pacific methods can lead to the realization of Communism are false friends to the wage-earners; intentionally or unintentionally, they are covert allies of the bourgeoisie.
There must, then, according to Bolshevik theory, be armed conflict sooner or later, if the injustices of the present economic system are ever to be remedied. Not only do they assume armed conflict: they have a fairly definite conception of the way in which it is to be conducted. This conception has been carried out in Russia, and is to be carried out, before very long, in every civilized country. The Communists, who represent the class-conscious wage-earners, wait for some propitious moment when events have caused a mood of revolutionary discontent with the existing Government. They then put themselves at the head of the discontent, carry through a successful revolution, and in so doing acquire the arms, the railways, the State treasure, and all the other resources upon which the power of modern Governments is built. They then confine political power to Communists, however small a minority they may be of the whole nation. They set to work to increase their number by propaganda and the control of education. And meanwhile, they introduce Communism into every department of economic life as quickly as possible.
Ultimately, after a longer or shorter period, according to circumstances, the nation will be converted to Communism, the relics of capitalist institutions will have been obliterated, and it will be possible to restore freedom.

===Chinese===

布尔什维克拥有一个实现共产主义（Communism）的非常明确的纲领——列宁（Lenin）曾一再阐述这一纲领，最近在第三国际（the Third International）对独立工党（Independent Labour Party）提交的问卷调查的答复中也有所体现。

我们被告知，资本家为了捍卫其特权将不择手段。只要阶级依然存在，为了本阶级的利益而战，就是人类在具备政治意识范围内的本性。当冲突未被推向极端时，妥协和政治欺骗的方法或许比实际的肉体战争更为可取；但一旦无产阶级对资本家发起真正致命的攻击，他们将遭到大炮和刺刀的还击。既然这是确凿且不可避免的，那么最好为此做好准备，并据此开展宣传。那些声称和平方法能够实现共产主义的人，是工薪劳动者的虚假朋友；无论是有意还是无意，他们都是资产阶级的隐蔽盟友。

因此，根据布尔什维克理论，如果要补救当前经济制度的不公，武装冲突迟早必然爆发。他们不仅预设了武装冲突的存在：他们对于武装冲突应当如何进行，还拥有相当明确的设想。这一设想已经在俄国付诸实践，并不久之后将在每一个文明国家付诸实践。代表有阶级意识的（class-conscious）工薪劳动者的共产党人，等待着事态引起对现存政府的革命性不满情绪的某个有利时机。随后，他们置身于这种不满情绪的前列，贯彻一场成功的革命，并在这一过程中夺取武器、铁路、国家财富以及现代政府权力所依仗的所有其他资源。随后，无论他们在全国人口中是多么微小的少数，他们都将政治权力局限于共产党员手中。他们着手通过宣传和控制教育来扩大自身的人数。与此同时，他们尽快将共产主义引入经济生活的每一个部门。

最终，在经历或长或短的一段时期后（视情况而定），全国人民都将皈依共产主义，资本主义制度的残余将被扫除殆尽，那时恢复自由便成为可能。

===Original===

But the political conflicts to which we are accustomed will not reappear. All the burning political questions of our time, according to the Communists, are questions of class conflict, and will disappear when the division of classes disappears. Accordingly the State will no longer be required, since the State is essentially an engine of power designed to give the victory to one side in the class conflict. Ordinary States are designed to give the victory to the capitalists; the proletarian State (Soviet Russia) is designed to give the victory to the wage-earners. As soon as the community contains only wage-earners, the State will cease to have any functions. And so, through a period of dictatorship, we shall finally arrive at a condition very similar to that aimed at by Anarchist Communism.
Three questions arise in regard to this method of reaching Utopia. First, would the ultimate state foreshadowed by the Bolsheviks be desirable in itself? Secondly, would the conflict involved in achieving it by the Bolshevik method be so bitter and prolonged that its evils would outweigh the ultimate good? Thirdly, is this method likely to lead, in the end, to the state which the Bolsheviks desire, or will it fail at some point and arrive at a quite different result? If we are to be Bolsheviks, we must answer all these questions in a sense favourable to their programme.
As regards the first question, I have no hesitation in answering it in a manner favourable to Communism. It is clear that the present inequalities of wealth are unjust. In part, they may be defended as affording an incentive to useful industry, but I do not think this defence will carry us very far. However, I have argued this question before in my book on *Roads to Freedom*, and I will not spend time upon it now. On this matter, I concede the Bolshevik case. It is the other two questions that I wish to discuss.
Our second question was: Is the ultimate good aimed at by the Bolsheviks sufficiently great to be worth the price that, according to their own theory, will have to be paid for achieving it?
If anything human were absolutely certain, we might answer this question affirmatively with some confidence.

===Chinese===

但我们所习以为常的政治冲突将不再重现。按照共产党人的说法，我们时代所有焦灼的政治问题都是阶级冲突的问题，一旦阶级划分消失，这些问题都将烟消云散。因此，国家将不再被需要，因为国家在本质上是一种旨在使阶级冲突中一方获得胜利的权力机器。普通国家旨在让资本家获胜；无产阶级国家（苏维埃俄国）旨在让工薪劳动者获胜。一旦社会只包含工薪劳动者，国家便将停止具有任何职能。于是，通过一段时期的专政，我们最终将达到一种与无政府主义共产主义所追求的目标极其相似的境况。

关于这种通往乌托邦的方法，产生了三个问题。首先，布尔什维克所预示的终极状态本身是否值得追求？其次，通过布尔什维克的方法达成这一状态所涉及的冲突，是否会如此激烈而漫长，以至于其弊端会压倒终极的善？第三，这种方法最终是否有可能引向布尔什维克所期望的状态，抑或它会在某一关头失败并导致截然不同的结果？如果我们要做布尔什维克，我们就必须在有利于其纲领的意义上回答所有这些问题。

关于第一个问题，我毫不犹豫地在有利于共产主义的意义上予以回答。显而易见，当前财富的不平等是不公正的。在某种程度上，它们可以被辩护为对有益实业提供了激励，但我认为这种辩护并不能走得很远。然而，我此前在《通往自由之路》（*Roads to Freedom*）一书中已经讨论过这一问题，现在我不想在此耗费时间。在这个问题上，我承认布尔什维克的论点。我希望讨论的是另外两个问题。

我们的第二个问题是：布尔什维克所追求的终极的善，是否值得付出其方法所必需付出的代价？

===Original===

The benefits of Communism, if it were once achieved, might be expected to be lasting; we might legitimately hope that further change would be towards something still better, not towards a revival of ancient evils. But if we admit, as we must do, that the outcome of the Communist revolution is in some degree uncertain, it becomes necessary to count the cost; for a great part of the cost is all but certain.
Since the revolution of October, 1917, the Soviet Government has been at war with almost all the world, and has had at the same time to face civil war at home. This is not to be regarded as accidental, or as a misfortune which could not be foreseen. According to Marxian theory, what has happened was bound to happen. Indeed, Russia has been wonderfully fortunate in not having to face an even more desperate situation. First and foremost, the world was exhausted by the war, and in no mood for military adventures. Next, the Tsarist regime was the worst in Europe, and therefore rallied less support than would be secured by any other capitalist Government. Again, Russia is vast and agricultural, making it capable of resisting both invasion and blockade better than Great Britain or France or Germany. The only other country that could have resisted with equal success is the United States, which is at present very far removed from a proletarian revolution, and likely long to remain the chief bulwark of the capitalist system. It is evident that Great Britain, attempting a similar revolution, would be forced by starvation to yield within a few months, provided America led a policy of blockade. The same is true, though in a less degree, of continental countries. Therefore, unless and until an international Communist revolution becomes possible, we must expect that any other nation following Russia’s example will have to pay an even higher price than Russia has had to pay.
Now the price that Russia is having to pay is very great. The almost universal poverty might be thought to be a small evil in comparison with the ultimate gain, but it brings with it other evils of which the magnitude would be acknowledged even by those who have never known poverty and therefore make light of it. Hunger brings an absorption in the question of food, which, to most people, makes life almost purely animal.

===Chinese===

共产主义的好处一旦实现，可以预期将是持久的；我们完全有理由希望进一步的改变将走向更加美好的事物，而非复活古老的罪恶。但是，如果我们承认——正如我们必须承认的那样——共产主义革命的结果在某种程度上是不确定的，那么计算代价就变得必不可少了；因为这笔代价的绝大部分几乎是确凿无疑的。

自 1917 年 10 月革命以来，苏维埃政府几乎与全世界都处于交战状态，并且同时不得不面对国内的内战。这绝不能被视为偶然，也不能被视为无法预见的厄运。根据马克思主义理论，已经发生的事情是注定要发生的。事实上，俄国能够免于面对更加绝望的境地，已经是极其幸运的了。首当其冲的是，全世界都被战争消耗得精疲力竭，毫无心思进行军事冒险。其次，沙皇政权是欧洲最糟糕的政权，因此它所能拉拢的支持远少于任何其他资本主义政府所能获得的支持。再者，俄国幅员辽阔且以农业为主，这使得它比英国、法国或德国更能抵抗侵略和封锁。唯一能够以同等成功进行抵抗的其他国家是美国，而美国目前距离无产阶级革命极其遥远，并且很可能长期充当资本主义制度的主要堡垒。显而易见，如果英国尝试类似的革命，只要美国主导封锁政策，英国在数月之内就会因饥荒而被迫屈服。大陆国家亦是如此，尽管程度稍轻。因此，除非并且直到国际共产主义革命成为可能，否则我们必须预料，任何效仿俄国榜样的其他国家都将不得不付出比俄国已经付出的代价还要高昂的代价。

如今俄国正在被迫付出的代价是极其巨大的。与终极收益相比，几乎普遍的贫困或许被认为是一个微不足道的恶，但它带来了其他恶果，这些恶果的严重程度即便是那些从未体会过贫困因而对其轻描淡写的人也不得不承认。饥饿导致人们全神贯注于食物问题，对大多数人而言，这使得生活几乎退化为纯粹的动物状态。

===Original===

The general shortage makes people fierce, and reacts upon the political atmosphere. The necessity of inculcating Communism produces a hothouse condition, where every breath of fresh air must be excluded: people are to be taught to think in a certain way, and all free intelligence becomes taboo. The country comes to resemble an immensely magnified Jesuit College. Every kind of liberty is banned as being “bourgeois”; but it remains a fact that intelligence languishes where thought is not free.
All this, however, according to the leaders of the Third International, is only a small beginning of the struggle, which must become worldwide before it achieves victory. In their reply to the Independent Labour Party they say:

> It is probable that upon the throwing off of the chains of the capitalist Governments, the revolutionary proletariat of Europe will meet the resistance of Anglo-Saxon capital in the persons of British and American capitalists who will attempt to blockade it. It is then possible that the revolutionary proletariat of Europe will rise in union with the peoples of the East and commence a revolutionary struggle, the scene of which will be the entire world, to deal a final blow to British and American capitalism (*The Times*, July 30, 1920).

===Chinese===

普遍的匮乏使人们变得凶狠残暴，并对政治氛围产生反冲作用。灌输共产主义的必要性造成了一种温室般的境况，每一丝新鲜空气都必须被隔绝在外：人们被教导要以某种特定方式思考，所有自由的心智都成了禁忌。这个国家变得宛如一座极大放大的耶稣会学院（Jesuit College）。任何形式的自由都因属于“资产阶级”而遭到取缔；但事实依然是：在思想不自由的地方，智力必然萎靡凋零。

然而，根据第三国际领导人的说法，所有这一切仅仅是这场斗争的微小开端，斗争在取得胜利之前必须走向世界范围。在对独立工党的答复中，他们写道：

> 很可能在挣脱资本主义政府的枷锁之时，欧洲革命无产阶级将遭遇以企图对其实施封锁的英美资本家为代表的盎格鲁-撒克逊资本势力的抵抗。届时，欧洲革命无产阶级很可能会与东方各族人民联合起来，掀起一场以整个世界为舞台的革命斗争，给英美资本主义以致命的打击（《泰晤士报》[*The Times*]，1920 年 7 月 30 日）。

===Original===

The war here prophesied, if it ever takes place, will be one compared to which the late war will come to seem a mere affair of outposts. Those who realize the destructiveness of the late war, the devastation and impoverishment, the lowering of the level of civilization throughout vast areas, the general increase of hatred and savagery, the letting loose of bestial instincts which had been curbed during peace﻿—those who realize all this will hesitate to incur inconceivably greater horrors, even if they believe firmly that Communism in itself is much to be desired. An economic system cannot be considered apart from the population which is to carry it out; and the population resulting from such a world-war as Moscow calmly contemplates would be savage, bloodthirsty and ruthless to an extent that must make any system a mere engine of oppression and cruelty.
This brings us to our third question: Is the system which Communists regard as their goal likely to result from the adoption of their methods? This is really the most vital question of the three.
Advocacy of Communism by those who believe in Bolshevik methods rests upon the assumption that there is no slavery except economic slavery, and that when all goods are held in common there must be perfect liberty. I fear this is a delusion.
There must be administration, there must be officials who control distribution. These men, in a Communist State, are the repositories of power. So long as they control the army, they are able, as in Russia at this moment, to wield despotic power even if they are a small minority. The fact that there is Communism﻿—to a certain extent﻿—does not mean that there is liberty. If the Communism were more complete, it would not necessarily mean more freedom; there would still be certain officials in control of the food supply, and these officials could govern as they pleased so long as they retained the support of the soldiers. This is not mere theory: it is the patent lesson of the present condition of Russia. The Bolshevik theory is that a small minority are to seize power, and are to hold it until Communism is accepted practically universally, which, they admit, may take a long time. But power is sweet, and few men surrender it voluntarily.

===Chinese===

在此所预言的战争一旦爆发，与之相比，最近这场大战将变得宛如一场前哨战。那些认识到最近这场战争的破坏性、认识到浩劫与赤贫、认识到广大地区文明水平的跌落、仇恨与残暴的普遍滋长，以及和平时期受到遏制的兽性本能被释放出来的人——那些认识到所有这一切的人，即便深信共产主义本身是极其值得追求的，也会在承受难以想象的更大恐怖面前退缩不前。一种经济体制不能脱离执行它的民众来单独考察；而从莫斯科泰然自若所构想的这样一场世界大战中产生出来的人口，其野蛮、嗜血与冷酷无情的程度，必将使任何体制都退化为纯粹压迫与残暴的机器。

这就引出了我们的第三个问题：共产党人视作目标的制度，是否有可能通过采纳其方法而产生？这确实是三个问题中最核心的问题。

那些信仰布尔什维克方法的人对共产主义的倡导，是建立在这样一种假定之上的：除了经济奴役之外不存在任何奴役，一旦所有财产共有，就必然享有完美的自由。恐怕这纯属幻觉。

必然需要管理机构，必然需要控制物资分配的官员。在共产主义国家，这些人就是权力的执掌者。只要他们控制着军队，即便他们只是少数人，他们也能够像此时在俄国一样行使专制权力。存在一定程度的共产主义这一事实，并不意味着存在自由。即便共产主义更加彻底，也并不必然意味着更多的自由；依然会有某些官员控制着粮食供应，而且只要这些官员保有士兵的支持，他们就可以为所欲为地进行统治。这不仅仅是理论：这是俄国当前状况昭然若揭的教训。布尔什维克的理论是由一小部分人夺取政权并掌权直到共产主义几乎被普遍接受，他们承认这可能需要很长时间。然而权力是甜美的，很少有人会自愿放弃权力。

===Original===

It is especially sweet to those who have the habit of it, and the habit becomes most ingrained in those who have governed by bayonets, without popular support. Is it not almost inevitable that men placed as the Bolsheviks are placed in Russia, and as they maintain that the Communists must place themselves wherever the social revolution succeeds, will be loath to relinquish their monopoly of power, and will find reasons for remaining until some new revolution ousts them? Would it not be fatally easy for them, without altering economic structure, to decree large salaries for high Government officials, and so reintroduce the old inequalities of wealth?
