What motive would they have for not doing so? What motive is possible except idealism, love of mankind, non-economic motives of the sort that Bolsheviks decry? The system created by violence and the forcible rule of a minority must necessarily allow of tyranny and exploitation; and if human nature is what Marxians assert it to be, why should the rulers neglect such opportunities of selfish advantage?
It is sheer nonsense to pretend that the rulers of a great empire such as Soviet Russia, when they have become accustomed to power, retain the proletarian psychology, and feel that their class-interest is the same as that of the ordinary working man. This is not the case in fact in Russia now, however the truth may be concealed by fine phrases. The Government has a class-consciousness and a class-interest quite distinct from those of the genuine proletarian, who is not to be confounded with the paper proletarian of the Marxian schema. In a capitalist state, the Government and the capitalists on the whole hang together, and form one class; in Soviet Russia, the Government has absorbed the capitalist mentality together with the governmental, and the fusion has given increased strength to the upper class. But I see no reason whatever to expect equality or freedom to result from such a system, except reasons derived from a false psychology and a mistaken analysis of the sources of political power.
I am compelled to reject Bolshevism for two reasons: First, because the price mankind must pay to achieve Communism by Bolshevik methods is too terrible; and secondly because, even after paying the price, I do not believe the result would be what the Bolsheviks profess to desire.

===Chinese===

对于那些已经习惯于权力的人而言，权力尤其甜美，而在那些依靠刺刀、在缺乏民意支持下进行统治的人身上，这种习惯变得最为根深蒂固。处在布尔什维克在俄国所处的地位上、以及正如他们坚称在社会革命取得成功的任何地方共产党人都必须处于的地位上的人，几乎不可避免地会不愿放弃其权力的垄断，并会寻找理由留任直到某场新的革命将他们驱逐出去，这难道不是近乎必然的吗？他们难道不会致命地轻易做到在不改变经济结构的情况下，为高级政府官员规定巨额薪金，从而重新引入旧有的财富不平等吗？他们有什么动机不去这么做？除了理想主义、对人类的热爱以及布尔什维克所鄙视的那类非经济动机之外，还能有什么可能的动机？由暴力和少数人的强权统治所建立起来的体制，必然容许专制与剥削；而且如果人性真如马克思主义者所断言的那样，统治者为何要忽视这种谋取私利的机会呢？

当像苏维埃俄国这样一个庞大帝国的统治者习惯了权力之后，硬说他们依然保留着无产阶级心理，觉得自己的阶级利益与普通工人是一样的，这完全是一派胡言。无论真理如何被华丽辞藻所掩盖，如今在俄国实际情况绝非如此。政府拥有与真正无产者截然不同的阶级意识和阶级利益，而真正的无产者决不可与马克思主义图式中纸面上的无产者混为一谈。在资本主义国家，政府与资本家总体上穿一条裤子，构成一个阶级；在苏维埃俄国，政府将资本家心态与统治者心态融为一体，这种融合赋予了上层阶级更大的力量。但我完全看不出有什么理由指望这样一种体制能带来平等或自由，除非这些理由源于虚假的心理学以及对政治权力来源的错误分析。

我不得不基于两个理由拒绝布尔什维主义：第一，人类为了通过布尔什维克方法实现共产主义所必须付出的代价太可怕了；第二，即便付出了这一代价，我也不相信结果会是布尔什维克声称所追求的目标。

===Original===

But if their methods are rejected, how are we ever to arrive at a better economic system? This is not an easy question, and I shall treat it in a separate chapter.

===Chinese===

但如果他们的方法遭到摒弃，我们究竟该如何走向更好的经济体制？这不是一个简单的问题，我将在单独的一章中予以论述。

## V Mechanism and the Individual / V 机械论与个人
===Original===

Is it possible to effect a fundamental reform of the existing economic system by any other method than that of Bolshevism? The difficulty of answering this question is what chiefly attracts idealists to the dictatorship of the proletariat. If, as I have argued, the method of violent revolution and Communist rule is not likely to have the results which idealists desire, we are reduced to despair unless we can see hope in other methods. The Bolshevik arguments against all other methods are powerful. I confess that, when the spectacle of present-day Russia forced me to disbelieve in Bolshevik methods, I was at first unable to see any way of curing the essential evils of capitalism. My first impulse was to abandon political thinking as a bad job, and to conclude that the strong and ruthless must always exploit the weaker and kindlier sections of the population. But this is not an attitude that can be long maintained by any vigorous and temperamentally hopeful person. Of course, if it were the truth, one would have to acquiesce. Some people believe that by living on sour milk one can achieve immortality. Such optimists are answered by a mere refutation; it is not necessary to go on and point out some other way of escaping death. Similarly an argument that Bolshevism will not lead to the millennium would remain valid even if it could be shown that the millennium cannot be reached by any other road. But the truth in social questions is not quite like truth in physiology or physics, since it depends upon men’s beliefs. Optimism tends to verify itself by making people impatient of avoidable evils; while despair, on the other hand, makes the world as bad as it believes it to be. It is therefore imperative for those who do not believe in Bolshevism to put some other hope in its place.
I think there are two things that must be admitted: first, that many of the worst evils of capitalism might survive under Communism; secondly, that the cure for these evils cannot be sudden, since it requires changes in the average mentality.
What are the chief evils of the present system? I do not think that mere inequality of wealth, in itself, is a very grave evil. If everybody had enough, the fact that some have more than enough would be unimportant.

===Chinese===

是否存在通过布尔什维主义（Bolshevism）之外的其他方法对现存经济制度进行根本变革的可能？回答这一问题的困难，正是将理想主义者主要吸引到无产阶级专政（dictatorship of the proletariat）旗帜下的原因。如果正如我所论证的那样，暴力革命与共产党统治的方法不太可能产生理想主义者所渴望的结果，那么除非我们能在其他方法中看到希望，否则我们将陷入绝望。布尔什维克反对所有其他方法的论点是强有力的。我坦承，当今日俄国的景象迫使我不再相信布尔什维克的方法时，我起初无法看出医治资本主义本质弊端的任何途径。我的第一冲动是将政治思考视为一项无望的事业而予以放弃，并得出结论：强横残暴者注定永远剥削人口中较为弱小和善良的部分。但这并不是任何充满生机且在性情上抱持希望的人能够长期维持的态度。当然，如果这就是事实，人们就不得不默认接受。有些人相信靠酸奶度日可以长生不老。对这种乐观主义者只需予以驳斥即可；没有必要进一步指出逃避死亡的某种其他途径。同样，证明布尔什维主义不会引向千禧盛世的论点，即便能够表明千禧盛世无法通过任何其他道路达成，也依然是有效的。然而社会问题中的真理并不完全像生理学或物理学中的真理，因为它取决于人们的信念。乐观主义往往通过让人们对可以避免的恶行无法容忍来证实自身；而另一方面，绝望则使世界变得如其所认为的那样糟糕。因此，对于那些不相信布尔什维主义的人而言，在它的位置上树立某种其他的希望是势在必行的。

我认为有两点必须承认：首先，资本主义许多最严重的弊端在共产主义（Communism）下完全可能继续存在；其次，根治这些弊端不可能一蹴而就，因为它需要普通人心智观念的转变。

当前制度的主要弊端是什么？我不认为单纯的财富不平等本身是一种非常严重的弊端。如果每个人都丰衣足食，某些人拥有超出所需的财富这一事实便无关紧要。

===Original===

With a very moderate improvement in methods of production, it would be easy to ensure that everybody should have enough, even under capitalism, if wars and preparations for wars were abolished. The problem of poverty is by no means insoluble within the existing system, except when account is taken of psychological factors and the uneven distribution of power.
The graver evils of the capitalist system all arise from its uneven distribution of power. The possessors of capital wield an influence quite out of proportion to their numbers or their services to the community. They control almost the whole of education and the press; they decide what the average man shall know or not know; the cinema has given them a new method of propaganda, by which they enlist the support of those who are too frivolous even for illustrated papers. Very little of the intelligence of the world is really free: most of it is, directly or indirectly, in the pay of business enterprises or wealthy philanthropists. To satisfy capitalist interests, men are compelled to work much harder and more monotonously than they ought to work, and their education is scamped. Wherever, as in barbarous or semi-civilized countries, labour is too weak or too disorganized to protect itself, appalling cruelties are practised for private profit. Economic and political organizations become more and more vast, leaving less and less room for individual development and initiative. It is this sacrifice of the individual to the machine that is the fundamental evil of the modern world.
To cure this evil is not easy, because efficiency is promoted, at any given moment, though not in the long run, by sacrificing the individual to the smooth working of a vast organization, whether military or industrial. In war and in commercial competition, it is necessary to control individual impulses, to treat men as so many “bayonets” or “sabres” or “hands,” not as a society of separate people with separate tastes and capacities. Some sacrifice of individual impulses is, of course, essential to the existence of an ordered community, and this degree of sacrifice is, as a rule, not regretable even from the individual’s point of view. But what is demanded in a highly militarized or industrialized nation goes far beyond this very moderate degree.

===Chinese===

只要在生产方法上取得非常适度的改良，即便在资本主义制度下，如果废除战争和军备竞赛，确保每个人衣食无忧也是轻而易举的。贫困问题在现存制度内绝非无法解决，除非考虑到心理因素以及权力的不均等分配。

资本主义制度更为严重的弊端，全部源于其权力的不均等分配。资本的拥有者所施加的影响，与其人数或其对社会的贡献完全不成比例。他们控制着几乎全部的教育和报刊；他们决定普通人应当知晓什么或不应当知晓什么；电影为他们提供了一种新的宣传手段，借助这种手段，他们把那些甚至对画报都嫌太严肃轻浮的人拉拢为支持者。世界上极少有真正自由的心智：绝大多数心智直接或间接地受雇于商业企业或富有的慈善家。为了满足资本家的利益，人们被迫从事比理应承担的远为繁重和单调的劳动，他们的教育被敷衍了事。凡是在劳工过于弱小或过于涣散而无法自保的地方——如在野蛮或半文明国家——为了个人私利就会实行骇人听闻的残酷行径。经济和政治组织变得越来越庞大，留给个人发展和首创精神的空间越来越狭窄。正是个人对机器（机械化体制）的这种牺牲，构成了现代世界的根本弊端。

根治这一弊端并非易事，因为在任何特定时刻——尽管从长远来看并非如此——通过为了庞大组织（无论是军事组织还是工业组织）的顺畅运转而牺牲个人，可以促进效率。在战争和商业竞争中，必须控制个人的冲动，将人视为一个个“刺刀”、“马刀”或“劳力”，而非视作由拥有各自品味和才能的独立个体构成的社会。当然，为了有序社会的存在，对个人冲动的某种牺牲是必不可少的，而且这种程度的牺牲通常来说即便从个人的角度来看也是无可遗憾的。但在一个高度军事化或工业化的国家中所要求的，远远超出了这种非常适度的程度。

===Original===

A society which is to allow much freedom to the individual must be strong enough to be not anxious about home defence, moderate enough to refrain from difficult external conquests, and rich enough to value leisure and a civilized existence more than an increase of consumable commodities.
But where the material conditions for such a state of affairs exist, the psychological conditions are not likely to exist unless power is very widely diffused throughout the community. Where power is concentrated in a few, it will happen, unless those few are very exceptional people, that they will value tangible achievements in the way of increase in trade or empire more than the slow and less obvious improvements that would result from better education combined with more leisure. The joys of victory are especially great to the holders of power, while the evils of a mechanical organization fall almost exclusively upon the less influential. For these reasons, I do not believe that any community in which power is much concentrated will long refrain from conflicts of the kind involving a sacrifice of what is most valuable in the individual. In Russia at this moment, the sacrifice of the individual is largely inevitable, because of the severity of the economic and military struggle. But I did not feel, in the Bolsheviks, any consciousness of the magnitude of this misfortune, or any realization of the importance of the individual as against the State. Nor do I believe that men who do realize this are likely to succeed, or to come to the top, in times when everything has to be done against personal liberty. The Bolshevik theory requires that every country, sooner or later, should go through what Russia is going through now. And in every country in such a condition we may expect to find the government falling into the hands of ruthless men, who have not by nature any love for freedom, and who will see little importance in hastening the transition from dictatorship to freedom. It is far more likely that such men will be tempted to embark upon new enterprises, requiring further concentration of forces, and postponing indefinitely the liberation of the populations which they use as their material.
For these reasons, equalization of wealth without equalization of power seems to me a rather small and unstable achievement.

===Chinese===

一个给予个人充分自由的社会，必须强大到无需担忧国土防卫，温和到克制不进行艰难的对外征服，并且富足到将闲暇与文明的存在看得重于消费品增加的程度。

但是在存在实现这种状态的物质条件的地方，除非权力在整个社会中非常广泛地普及分散，否则心理条件是不太可能存在的。在权力集中于少数人手中的地方，除非这少数人是极其非同寻常的人，否则他们往往会将以贸易或帝国扩张形式呈现的有形成就，看得重于由更好的教育结合更多的闲暇所带来的缓慢且不那么明显的改善。胜利的喜悦对于权力的执掌者而言尤为巨大，而机械化组织的恶果几乎完全落在了影响力较小的人身上。基于这些理由，我不相信任何权力高度集中的社会能够长期避免涉及牺牲个人最宝贵价值的那类冲突。目前在俄国，由于经济和军事斗争的严峻残酷性，个人的牺牲在很大程度上是不可避免的。但在布尔什维克身上，我并未感受到对这一不幸严重性的任何意识，也未看到对个人相对于国家之重要性的任何认识。我亦不相信那些确实认识到这一点的人，在一切都不得不违背个人自由行事的时期，能够取得成功或脱颖而出。布尔什维克理论要求每个国家迟早都要经历俄国目前正在经历的一切。而且在处于这种境况的每一个国家中，我们都可以预料政府将落入生性并不热爱自由的冷酷无情者之手，他们认为加速从专政向自由过渡微不足道。更有可能的是，这些人将被诱惑去从事需要进一步集中力量的新事业，并无限期推迟把他们用作原料的人民的解放。

基于这些理由，在我看来，没有权力均等化的财富均等化是一项相当微小且不稳固的成就。

===Original===

But equalization of power is not a thing that can be achieved in a day. It requires a considerable level of moral, intellectual, and technical education. It requires a long period without extreme crises, in order that habits of tolerance and good nature may become common. It requires vigour on the part of those who are acquiring power, without a too desperate resistance on the part of those whose share is diminishing. This is only possible if those who are acquiring power are not very fierce, and do not terrify their opponents by threats of ruin and death. It cannot be done quickly, because quick methods require that very mechanism and subordination of the individual which we should struggle to prevent.
But even equalization of power is not the whole of what is needed politically. The right grouping of men for different purposes is also essential. Self-government in industry, for example, is an indispensable condition of a good society. Those acts of an individual or a group which have no very great importance for outsiders ought to be freely decided by that individual or group. This is recognized as regards religion, but ought to be recognized over a much wider field.
Bolshevik theory seems to me to err by concentrating its attention upon one evil, namely inequality of wealth, which it believes to be at the bottom of all others. I do not believe any one evil can be thus isolated, but if I had to select one as the greatest of political evils, I should select inequality of power. And I should deny that this is likely to be cured by the class-war and the dictatorship of the Communist party. Only peace and a long period of gradual improvement can bring it about.
Good relations between individuals, freedom from hatred and violence and oppression, general diffusion of education, leisure rationally employed, the progress of art and science﻿—these seem to me among the most important ends that a political theory ought to have in view. I do not believe that they can be furthered, except very rarely, by revolution and war; and I am convinced that at the present moment they can only be promoted by a diminution in the spirit of ruthlessness generated by the war.

===Chinese===

但权力的均等化并非一朝一夕所能实现。它需要相当水平的道德、智力和技术教育。它需要一个没有极端危机的漫长时期，以便宽容与善良的习惯能够走向普及。它需要那些正在获取权力的人充满活力，同时那些份额正在减少的人不至于进行过于绝望的抵抗。唯有在正在获取权力的人不过于凶暴、且不用毁灭和死亡的威胁恐吓对手的前提下，这才是可能的。它不可能迅速达成，因为迅速的方法恰恰需要我们竭力防止的那种机制化（机械论）以及对个人的屈从。

但即便权力的均等化，也并非政治上所需的一切。出于不同目的将人进行恰当的组合也是必不可少的。例如，工业自治是一个美好社会不可或缺的前提。个人或群体的那些对局外人没有重大影响的行为，理应由该个人或群体自由决断。这在宗教领域已为人所承认，但应当在广泛得多的领域中得到承认。

在我看来，布尔什维克理论的谬误在于将注意力集中在单一弊端上，即财富不平等，并认为财富不平等是一切其他弊端的根源。我不相信任何单一弊端可以如此被孤立看待，但如果我必须挑选出最大的政治弊端，我将选择权力的不平等。而且我否认这能够通过阶级战争和共产党的专政得到医治。唯有和平以及漫长时期的逐步改善才能带来这一结果。

人与人之间的良好关系、免于仇恨、暴力和压迫的自由、教育的普遍普及、合理利用的闲暇、艺术与科学的进步——在我看来，这些都属于政治理论理应着眼的极为重要的目标。我不相信革命和战争能够促进这些目标（除极罕见情况外）；而且我深信，在眼下这个关头，唯有通过削弱由战争所激发的冷酷无情精神，才能推进这些目标。

===Original===

For these reasons, while admitting the necessity and even utility of Bolshevism in Russia, I do not wish to see it spread, or to encourage the adoption of its philosophy by advanced parties in the Western nations.

===Chinese===

基于这些理由，尽管我承认布尔什维主义在俄国的必要性乃至有用性，我却不希望看到它蔓延，亦不鼓励西方国家的先进政党采纳其哲学。

## VI Why Russian Communism Has Failed / VI 为何俄国共产主义遭致失败
===Original===

The civilized world seems almost certain, sooner or later, to follow the example of Russia in attempting a Communist organization of society. I believe that the attempt is essential to the progress and happiness of mankind during the next few centuries, but I believe also that the transition has appalling dangers. I believe that, if the Bolshevik theory as to the method of transition is adopted by Communists in Western nations, the result will be a prolonged chaos, leading neither to Communism nor to any other civilized system, but to a relapse into the barbarism of the Dark Ages. In the interests of Communism, no less than in the interests of civilization, I think it imperative that the Russian failure should be admitted and analysed. For this reason, if for no other, I cannot enter into the conspiracy of concealment which many Western Socialists who have visited Russia consider necessary.
I shall try first to recapitulate the facts which make me regard the Russian experiment as a failure, and then to seek out the causes of failure.
The most elementary failure in Russia is in regard to food. In a country which formerly produced a vast exportable surplus of cereals and other agricultural produce, and in which the non-agricultural population is only fifteen percent of the total, it ought to be possible, without great difficulty, to provide enough food for the towns. Yet the Government has failed badly in this respect. The rations are inadequate and irregular, so that it is impossible to preserve health and vigour without the help of food purchased illicitly in the markets at speculative prices. I have given reasons for thinking that the breakdown of transport, though a contributory cause, is not the main reason for the shortage. The main reason is the hostility of the peasants, which, in turn, is due to the collapse of industry and to the policy of forced requisitions. In regard to corn and flour, the Government requisitions all that the peasant produces above a certain minimum required for himself and his family. If, instead, it exacted a fixed amount as rent, it would not destroy his incentive to production, and would not provide nearly such a strong motive for concealment. But this plan would have enabled the peasants to grow rich, and would have involved a confessed abandonment of Communist theory.

===Chinese===

文明世界迟早几乎肯定会效仿俄国的榜样，尝试对社会进行共产主义（Communism）组织。我相信这一尝试对于未来几百年人类的进步与幸福是不可或缺的，但我也相信这种过渡伴随着可怕的危险。我相信，如果西方国家的共产党人采纳布尔什维克关于过渡方法的理论，其结果将是旷日持久的混乱，既不会引向共产主义，也不会引向任何其他文明制度，而是会倒退回黑暗时代的野蛮状态。不仅为了文明的利益，同样为了共产主义本身的利益，我认为承认并分析俄国的失败是势在必行的。即便没有其他原因，单凭这一条，我也无法参与许多访问过俄国的西方社会主义者认为有必要的隐瞒共谋。

我将首先尝试概述使我认为俄国实验归于失败的事实，进而探寻其失败的原因。

俄国最基本的失败在于粮食方面。在一个曾经生产大量可供出口的剩余谷物和其他农产品、且非农业人口仅占总人口百分之十五的国家，为城市提供充足的粮食理应是不费吹灰之力就能做到的。然而政府在这方面却严重失败了。配给不仅不足而且极不规律，以至于如果不借助在市场上以投机价格非法购买的食物，就根本无法保持健康与活力。我已经陈述了理由，说明运输系统的崩溃虽然是一个促成原因，却并非粮食匮乏的主要原因。主要原因在于农民的敌意，而这反过来又是由于工业的崩溃以及余粮强征政策造成的。关于粮食和面粉，政府征集农民生产的超出其自身及其家庭所需最低限额的全部剩余。相反，如果政府征收固定数额作为地租，就不会破坏农民的生产积极性，也不会提供如此强烈的瞒报动机。但这一方案本会让农民富裕起来，并且意味着公开放弃共产主义理论。

===Original===

It has therefore been thought better to employ forcible methods, which led to disaster, as they were bound to do.
The collapse of industry was the chief cause of the food difficulties, and has in turn been aggravated by them. Owing to the fact that there is abundant food in the country, industrial and urban workers are perpetually attempting to abandon their employment for agriculture. This is illegal, and is severely punished, by imprisonment or convict labour. Nevertheless it continues, and in so vast a country as Russia it is not possible to prevent it. Thus the ranks of industry become still further depleted.
Except as regards munitions of war, the collapse of industry in Russia is extraordinarily complete. The resolutions passed by the Ninth Congress of the Communist Party (April, 1920) speak of “the incredible catastrophes of public economy.” This language is not too strong, though the recovery of the Baku oil has done something to produce a revival along the Volga basin.
The failure of the whole industrial side of the national economy, including transport, is at the bottom of the other failures of the Soviet Government. It is, to begin with, the main cause of the unpopularity of the Communists both in town and country: in town, because the people are hungry; in the country, because food is taken with no return except paper. If industry had been prosperous, the peasants could have had clothes and agricultural machinery, for which they would have willingly parted with enough food for the needs of the towns. The town population could then have subsisted in tolerable comfort; disease could have been coped with, and the general lowering of vitality averted. It would not have been necessary, as it has been in many cases, for men of scientific or artistic capacity to abandon the pursuits in which they were skilled for unskilled manual labour. The Communist Republic might have been agreeable to live in﻿—at least for those who had been very poor before.
The unpopularity of the Bolsheviks, which is primarily due to the collapse of industry, has in turn been accentuated by the measures which it has driven the Government to adopt.

===Chinese===

因此，当局认为采用强迫手段更好，而这必然导致了灾难，正如其所注定发生的那样。

工业的崩溃是粮食困难的主要原因，反过来又被粮食困难进一步加剧。由于农村有丰富的食物，工业和城市工人不断企图放弃工作去务农。这是非法的，并会受到监禁或服苦役的严厉惩处。然而这种现象仍在继续，在俄国这样一个辽阔的国家，要防止这一点是不可能的。于是工业队伍进一步枯竭。

除了军火工业之外，俄国工业的崩溃是异乎寻常地彻底。共产党第九次代表大会（1920 年 4 月）通过的决议谈到了“国民经济令人难以置信的灾难”。这种措辞绝非过分，尽管收复巴库石油在伏尔加河流域产生了一定程度的复苏。

包括运输在内的国家经济整个工业层面的失败，是苏维埃政府其他一切失败的根源。首先，它是共产党人在城市和乡村都不得人心的主要原因：在城市是因为人们在挨饿；在农村是因为粮食被拿走却换不来任何东西唯有纸币。如果工业繁荣，农民就可以得到衣物和农业机械，为此他们本会乐意交出足够满足城市需求的粮食。城市人口本可以生活在尚能忍受的舒适之中；疾病本可以得到控制，整体生命活力的衰落亦可避免。从事科学或艺术才能的人也就没有必要像在许多情况下那样，被迫放弃自己擅长的志业去从事非熟练的体力劳动。共产主义共和国本可以成为一个宜居之所——至少对于那些以前极度贫困的人来说是如此。

===Original===

In view of the fact that it was impossible to give adequate food to the ordinary population of Petrograd and Moscow, the Government decided that at any rate the men employed on important public work should be sufficiently nourished to preserve their efficiency. It is a gross libel to say that the Communists, or even the leading People’s Commissaries, live luxurious lives according to our standards; but it is a fact that they are not exposed, like their subjects, to acute hunger and the weakening of energy that accompanies it. No tone can blame them for this, since the work of government must be carried on; but it is one of the ways in which class distinctions have reappeared where it was intended that they should be banished. I talked to an obviously hungry working man in Moscow, who pointed to the Kremlin and remarked: “In there they have enough to eat.” He was expressing a widespread feeling which is fatal to the idealistic appeal that Communism attempts to make.
Owing to unpopularity, the Bolsheviks have had to rely upon the army and the Extraordinary Commission, and have been compelled to reduce the Soviet system to an empty form. More and more the pretence of representing the proletariat has grown threadbare. Amid official demonstrations and processions and meetings the genuine proletarian looks on, apathetic and disillusioned, unless he is possessed of unusual energy and fire, in which case he looks to the ideas of syndicalism or the I.W.W. to liberate him from a slavery far more complete than that of capitalism. A sweated wage, long hours, industrial conscription, prohibition of strikes, prison for slackers, diminution of the already insufficient rations in factories where the production falls below what the authorities expect, an army of spies ready to report any tendency to political disaffection and to procure imprisonment for its promoters﻿—this is the reality of a system which still professes to govern in the name of the proletariat.
At the same time the internal and external peril has necessitated the creation of a vast army recruited by conscription, except as regards a Communist nucleus, from among a population utterly weary of war, who put the Bolsheviks in power because they alone promised peace.

===Chinese===

鉴于无法向彼得格勒（Petrograd）和莫斯科（Moscow）的普通居民提供充足的粮食，政府决定无论如何应当让从事重要公共工作的人获得充分的营养以保持其工作效率。按照我们的标准说共产党人甚至主要的人民委员过着奢侈的生活，是一种恶意的诽谤；但事实是，他们并没有像其臣民那样遭受剧烈的饥饿以及伴随而来的精力衰竭。没有人能为此责备他们，因为政府的工作必须维持下去；但这正是阶级区分在原本旨在消除它的地方重新出现的途径之一。我曾与莫斯科一位明显处于饥饿之中的工人交谈，他指着克里姆林宫评论道：“在那里面，他们有足够吃的东西。”他表达了一种普遍的情感，而这种情感对于共产主义企图发出的理想主义号召是致命的。

由于不得民心，布尔什维克不得不依赖军队和契卡（Cheka，全俄肃反委员会），并被迫将苏维埃制度沦为一种空洞的形式。代表无产阶级的幌子变得越来越破绽百出。在官方的示威、游行和集会之中，真正的无产者在一旁冷眼旁观，漠然而幻灭，除非他拥有非同寻常的精力和热情，在这种情况下，他就会寄希望于工团主义（syndicalism）或世界产业工人联合会（I.W.W.）的思想，以将自己从一种远比资本主义更彻底的奴役中解放出来。微薄的血汗工资、漫长的工时、工业劳动征用制、严禁罢工、对怠工者的监禁、在产量低于当局预期的工厂中削减本已不足的配给、大批随时准备汇报任何政治不满倾向并将其鼓动者投入监狱的密探——这就是这个依然声称以无产阶级名义进行统治的体制的现实。

与此同时，内部和外部的危险使得组建一支庞大的军队成为必要，这支军队除了共产党员的核心骨干之外，是通过征兵从极度厌战的人口中招募而来的，而这群人之所以让布尔什维克上台，仅仅是因为他们唯独许诺了和平。

===Original===

Militarism has produced its inevitable result in the way of a harsh and dictatorial spirit: the men in power go through their day’s work with the consciousness that they command three million armed men, and that civilian opposition to their will can be easily crushed.
Out of all this has grown a system painfully like the old government of the Tsar﻿—a system which is Asiatic in its centralized bureaucracy, its secret service, its atmosphere of governmental mystery and submissive terror. In many ways it resembles our Government of India. Like that Government, it stands for civilization, for education, sanitation, and Western ideas of progress; it is composed in the main of honest and hardworking men, who despise those whom they govern, but believe themselves possessed of something valuable which they must communicate to the population, however little it may be desired. Like our Government in India, they live in terror of popular risings, and are compelled to resort to cruel repressions in order to preserve their power. Like it, they represent an alien philosophy of life, which cannot be forced upon the people without a change of instinct, habit, and tradition so profound as to dry up the vital springs of action, producing listlessness and despair among the ignorant victims of militant enlightenment. It may be that Russia needs sternness and discipline more than anything else; it may be that a revival of Peter the Great’s methods is essential to progress. From this point of view, much of what it is natural to criticize in the Bolsheviks becomes defensible; but this point of view has little affinity to Communism. Bolshevism may be defended, possibly, as a dire discipline through which a backward nation is to be rapidly industrialized; but as an experiment in Communism it has failed.
There are two things that a defender of the Bolsheviks may say against the argument that they have failed because the present state of Russia is bad. It may be said that it is too soon to judge, and it may be urged that whatever failure there has been is attributable to the hostility of the outside world.
As to the contention that it is too soon to judge, that is of course undeniable in a sense. But in a sense it is always too soon to judge of any historical movement, because its effects and developments go on forever. Bolshevism has, no doubt, great changes ahead of it.

===Chinese===

军国主义在严酷和专制的精神方面产生了其不可避免的结果：当权者在日常工作中深知自己统率着三百万武装人员，民间的反对意见可以被轻而易举地碾碎。

从所有这一切之中，滋生出一种令人痛苦地类似于沙皇旧政府的体制——一种在中央集权官僚体制、特务机构、政府神秘氛围以及顺从恐惧方面纯属亚洲式的体制。在许多方面，它类似于我们在印度的统治当局。正如那个政府一样，它代表着文明、教育、公共卫生和西方的进步观念；它主要由诚实勤勉的人组成，他们鄙视其所统治的人民，却坚信自己掌握着某种宝贵的东西，必须将其传授给人民，无论人民多么不情愿接受。正如我们在印度的政府一样，他们生活在对民众起义的恐惧之中，被迫诉诸残酷的镇压手段以维系其统治。正如它一样，他们代表着一种外来的生活哲学，如果不发生深刻到足以抽干生机勃勃行动源泉的本能、习惯与传统变革，这种哲学就无法强加给人民，从而在好战启蒙的无知受害者中产生倦怠与绝望。也许俄国最需要的就是严苛与纪律；也许彼得大帝手法的复活对于进步是必不可少的。从这一角度来看，在布尔什维克身上很自然受到批评的许多东西就变得情有可原了；但这一观点与共产主义几乎毫无共同之处。布尔什维主义或许可以作为一种使落后国家迅速工业化的残酷纪律而得到辩护；但作为一次共产主义的实验，它已经失败了。

布尔什维克的辩护者可能会针对他们因俄国现状恶劣而归于失败的论点提出两点反驳。有人可能会说现在下结论为时尚早，也有人可能会坚称任何失败都归咎于外部世界的敌意。

至于现在下结论为时过早的论调，在某种意义上当然是不可否认的。但在某种意义上，对任何历史运动下结论永远都为时过早，因为其影响与演变是无休止延续的。布尔什维克未来无疑面临着巨大的变革。

===Original===

But the last three years have afforded material for some judgments, though more definitive judgments will be possible later. And, for reasons which I have given in earlier chapters, I find it impossible to believe that later developments will realize more fully the Communist ideal. If trade is opened with the outer world, there will be an almost irresistible tendency to resumption of private enterprise. If trade is not reopened, the plans of Asiatic conquest will mature, leading to a revival of Genghis Khan and Timur. In neither case is the purity of the Communist faith likely to survive.
As for the hostility of the Entente, it is of course true that Bolshevism might have developed very differently if it had been treated in a friendly spirit. But in view of its desire to promote world-revolution, no one could expect﻿—and the Bolsheviks certainly did not expect﻿—that capitalist Governments would be friendly. If Germany had won the war, Germany would have shown a hostility more effective than that of the Entente. However we may blame Western Governments for their policy, we must realize that, according to the deterministic economic theory of the Bolsheviks, no other policy was to be expected from them. Other men might have been excused for not foreseeing the attitude of Churchill, Clemenceau and Millerand; but Marxians could not be excused, since this attitude was in exact accord with their own formula.
We have seen the symptoms of Bolshevik failure; I come now to the question of its profounder causes.
Everything that is worst in Russia we found traceable to the collapse of industry. Why has industry collapsed so utterly? And would it collapse equally if a Communist revolution were to occur in a Western country?
Russian industry was never highly developed, and depended always upon outside aid for much of its plant. The hostility of the world, as embodied in the blockade, left Russia powerless to replace the machinery and locomotives worn out during the war. The need of self-defence compelled the Bolsheviks to send their best workmen to the front, because they were the most reliable Communists, and the loss of them rendered their factories even more inefficient than they were under Kerensky. In this respect, and in the laziness and incapacity of the Russian workman, the Bolsheviks have had to face special difficulties which would be less in other countries.

===Chinese===

但过去三年已经为某些判断提供了素材，尽管更具决定性的判断在日后才有可能做出。而且，基于我在前面章节中陈述的理由，我发现无法相信以后的演变会更加充分地实现共产主义理想。如果与外部世界开启贸易，就会产生一种几乎不可抗拒的恢复私营企业的倾向。如果贸易不重新开放，亚洲征服的计划就会成熟，导致成吉思汗与帖木儿的复活。在这两种情况下，共产主义信仰的纯洁性都不太可能幸存下来。

至于协约国（Entente）的敌意，诚然，如果布尔什维主义受到友好对待，它的发展可能会大不相同。但鉴于其推进世界革命的愿望，没有人能够指望——布尔什维克当然也没有指望——资本主义政府会友好相待。如果德国赢得了战争，德国所展现出的敌意将比协约国更具破坏力。无论我们如何因西方政府的政策而谴责他们，我们必须认识到，根据布尔什维克的经济决定论理论，本就无法指望他们采取任何其他政策。其他人如果未能预见到丘吉尔（Churchill）、克列孟梭（Clemenceau）和米勒兰（Millerand）的态度或许情有可原；但马克思主义者是无可开脱的，因为这种态度与他们自己的公式完全吻合。

我们已经看到了布尔什维克失败的表象；我现在来谈谈其更深层原因的问题。

我们在俄国发现的最糟糕的一切，都可以追溯到工业的崩溃。工业为何崩溃得如此彻底？如果西方国家爆发共产主义革命，工业是否也会同样崩溃？

俄国工业从未高度发达过，其大量厂房设备始终依赖外部援助。体现为封锁（blockade）的世界敌意，使俄国无力更换战争期间磨损的机器和机车。自卫的需要迫使布尔什维克将最优秀的工人送往前线，因为他们是最可靠的共产党员，而失去他们使工厂比在克伦斯基统治下更加缺乏效率。在这方面，以及在俄国工人的怠惰与无能方面，布尔什维克不得不面对特殊的困难，而这些困难在其他国家会相对较轻。

===Original===

On the other hand, they have had special advantages in the fact that Russia is self-supporting in the matter of food; no other country could have endured the collapse of industry so long, and no other Great Power except the United States could have survived years of blockade.
The hostility of the world was in no way a surprise to those who made the October revolution; it was in accordance with their general theory, and its consequences should have been taken into account in making the revolution.
Other hostilities besides those of the outside world have been incurred by the Bolsheviks with open eyes, notably the hostility of the peasants and that of a great part of the industrial population. They have attempted, in accordance with their usual contempt for conciliatory methods, to substitute terror for reward as the incentive to work. Some amiable Socialists have imagined that, when the private capitalist had been eliminated, men would work from a sense of obligation to the community. The Bolsheviks will have none of such sentimentalism. In one of the resolutions of the ninth Communist Congress they say:

===Chinese===

另一方面，他们拥有特殊的优势，即俄国在粮食方面是自给自足的；没有其他国家能够承受如此长期的工业崩溃，而且除了美国之外，没有任何其他大国能够在长达数年的封锁中幸存下来。

对于发动十月革命的人来说，全世界的敌意绝非意外之举；这完全符合他们的一般理论，而且在发动革命时理应将这一后果考虑在内。

除了外部世界的敌意之外，布尔什维克还是睁大着双眼招致了其他敌意，特别是农民的敌意以及相当一部分工业人口的敌意。按照他们对调解方法惯常的蔑视，他们企图用恐惧替代奖赏作为劳动的激励。一些善良的社会主义者曾设想，在消灭了私人资本家之后，人们将出于对社会的责任感而工作。布尔什维克对这种感伤主义嗤之以鼻。在第九次共产党代表大会的一项决议中，他们指出：

===Original===

> Every social system, whether based on slavery, feudalism, or capitalism, had its ways and means of labour compulsion and labour education in the interests of the exploiters.
> The Soviet system is faced with the task of developing its own methods of labour compulsion to attain an increase of the intensity and wholesomeness of labour; this method is to be based on the socialization of public economy in the interests of the whole nation.
> In addition to the propaganda by which the people are to be influenced and the repressions which are to be applied to all idlers, parasites and disorganizers who strive to undermine public zeal﻿—the principal method for the increase of production will become the introduction of the system of compulsory labour.
> In capitalist society rivalry assumed the character of competition and led to the exploitation of man by man. In a society where the means of production are nationalized, labour rivalry is to increase the products of labour without infringing its solidarity.
> Rivalry between factories, regions, guilds, workshops, and individual workers should become the subject of careful organization and of close study on the side of the Trade Unions and the economic organs.
> The system of premiums which is to be introduced should become one of the most powerful means of exciting rivalry. The system of rationing of food supply is to get into line with it; so long as Soviet Russia suffers from insufficiency of provisions, it is only just that the industrious and conscientious worker receives more than the careless worker.

===Chinese===

“任何社会制度，无论建立在奴隶制、封建制还是资本主义的基础之上，为了剥削者的利益，都拥有其强迫劳动和劳动教育的途径与方法。

苏维埃制度面临着发展自身强迫劳动方法以实现劳动强度和健全性增长的任务；这一方法必须建立在为了全体人民利益对国民经济实行社会化的基础之上。

除了用以影响人民的宣传，以及针对所有企图破坏公众热忱的懒汉、寄生虫和破坏分子所适用的镇压措施之外——提高生产的主要方法将是实行强迫劳动制度。

在资本主义社会，对抗采取了竞争的形式，导致了人对人的剥削。在生产资料收归国有的社会中，劳动竞赛应当在不损害其团结的前提下增加劳动产出。

工厂、地区、行会、车间以及单个工人之间的竞赛，应当成为工会和经济机关精心组织和深入研究的课题。

即将引入的奖励制度应当成为激发竞赛的最有力手段之一。粮食配给制度必须与此步调一致；只要苏维埃俄国仍在承受粮食不足的困扰，勤勉负责的工人比粗心大意的工人得到更多的配给，就是完全公正的。”

===Original===

It must be remembered that even the “industrious and conscientious worker” receives less food than is required to maintain efficiency.
Over the whole development of Russia and of Bolshevism since the October revolution there broods a tragic fatality. In spite of outward success the inner failure has proceeded by inevitable stages﻿—stages which could, by sufficient acumen, have been foreseen from the first. By provoking the hostility of the outside world the Bolsheviks were forced to provoke the hostility of the peasants, and finally the hostility or utter apathy of the urban and industrial population. These various hostilities brought material disaster, and material disaster brought spiritual collapse. The ultimate source of the whole train of evils lies in the Bolshevik outlook on life: in its dogmatism of hatred and its belief that human nature can be completely transformed by force. To injure capitalists is not the ultimate goal of Communism, though among men dominated by hatred it is the part that gives zest to their activities. To face the hostility of the world may show heroism, but it is a heroism for which the country, not its rulers, has to pay the price. In the principles of Bolshevism there is more desire to destroy ancient evils than to build up new goods; it is for this reason that success in destruction has been so much greater than in construction. The desire to destroy is inspired by hatred, which is not a constructive principle. From this essential characteristic of Bolshevik mentality has sprung the willingness to subject Russia to its present martyrdom. It is only out of a quite different mentality that a happier world can be created.
And from this follows a further conclusion. The Bolshevik outlook is the outcome of the cruelty of the Tsarist regime and the ferocity of the years of the Great War, operating upon a ruined and starving nation maddened into universal hatred. If a different mentality is needed for the establishment of a successful Communism, then a quite different conjuncture must see its inauguration; men must be persuaded to the attempt by hope, not driven to it by despair. To bring this about should be the aim of every Communist who desires the happiness of mankind more than the punishment of capitalists and their governmental satellites.

===Chinese===

必须记住的是，即便“勤勉负责的工人”所得到的食物，也低于维持工作效率所需的水平。

在十月革命以来俄国和布尔什维主义的整个发展历程中，笼罩着一层悲剧性的宿命色彩。尽管表面取得了成功，内在的失败却沿着不可避免的阶段逐步推进——只要具备足够的敏锐度，这些阶段从一开始就是可以预见的。通过激起外部世界的敌意，布尔什维克被迫激起农民的敌意，最终激起城市和工业人口的敌意或彻底的冷漠。这些形形色色的敌意带来了物质灾难，而物质灾难又带来了精神崩溃。这一整串罪恶的终极根源，在于布尔什维克的人生观：在于其仇恨的教条主义，以及认为人性可以通过武力彻底改造的信念。伤害资本家并不是共产主义的终极目标，尽管在受仇恨支配的人中间，这正是赋予其活动以热忱的部分。面对全世界的敌意或许展现了英雄主义，但这种英雄主义的代价却必须由国家而非其统治者来承担。在布尔什维主义的原则中，毁灭旧恶的渴望多于建设新善的追求；正因如此，破坏上的成功远大于建设上的成就。毁灭的渴望是由仇恨激发的，而仇恨并不是一种建设性的原则。正是从布尔什维克心态的这一本质特征中，滋生出使俄国承受当下苦难的甘心情愿。唯有从一种截然不同的心态中，才能创造出一个更幸福的世界。

由此得出了进一步的结论。布尔什维克的世界观是沙皇政权的残酷以及大战各年份的凶暴的产物，它作用于一个陷入绝境、处于饥饿之中且被逼得走向普遍仇恨的国家。如果要建立成功的共产主义需要一种不同的心态，那么它的开创就必须见证于一种完全不同的时机；必须通过希望引导人们去尝试，而非通过绝望驱使人们去冒险。促成这一局面的出现，应当成为每一位将人类幸福看得重于惩罚资本家及其政府走狗的共产党员的目标。

## VII Conditions for the Success of Communism / VII 共产主义成功的条件
===Original===

The fundamental ideas of Communism are by no means impracticable, and would, if realized, add immeasurably to the well-being of mankind. The difficulties which have to be faced are not in regard to the fundamental ideas, but in regard to the transition from capitalism. It must be assumed that those who profit by the existing system will fight to preserve it, and their fight may be sufficiently severe to destroy all that is best in Communism during the struggle, as well as everything else that has value in modern civilization. The seriousness of this problem of transition is illustrated by Russia, and cannot be met by the methods of the Third International. The Soviet Government, at the present moment, is anxious to obtain manufactured goods from capitalist countries, but the Third International is meanwhile endeavouring to promote revolutions which, if they occurred, would paralyse the industries of the countries concerned, and leave them incapable of supplying Russian needs.
The supreme condition of success in a Communist revolution is that it should not paralyse industry. If industry is paralysed, the evils which exist in modern Russia, or others just as great, seem practically unavoidable. There will be the problem of town and country, there will be hunger, there will be fierceness and revolts and military tyranny. All these things follow in a fatal sequence; and the end of them is almost certain to be something quite different from what genuine Communists desire.
If industry is to survive throughout a Communist revolution, a number of conditions must be fulfilled which are not, at present, fulfilled anywhere. Consider, for the sake of definiteness, what would happen if a Communist revolution were to occur in England tomorrow. Immediately America would place an embargo on all trade with us. The cotton industry would collapse, leaving about five million of the most productive portion of the population idle. The food supply would become inadequate, and would fail disastrously if, as is to be expected, the Navy were hostile or disorganized by the sabotage of the officers. The result would be that, unless there were a counterrevolution, about half the population would die within the first twelve months. On such a basis it would evidently be impossible to erect a successful Communist State.

===Chinese===

共产主义（Communism）的根本思想绝非不可行，而且如果得以实现，将无量地增进人类的福祉。必须面对的困难不在于根本思想，而在于从资本主义的过渡阶段。必须假定，那些从现存制度中获利的人将为维护该制度而战，他们的战斗可能会激烈到在斗争过程中摧毁共产主义中一切最美好的东西，以及现代文明中具有价值的一切其他事物。这一过渡问题的严重性在俄国得到了说明，且无法通过第三国际（the Third International）的方法来应对。苏维埃（Soviet）政府眼下正急于从资本主义国家获取工业品，而与此同时第三国际却在竭力推进革命，这些革命一旦爆发，将使相关国家的工业陷入瘫痪，从而使它们无力供应俄国的需求。

共产主义革命取得成功的首要条件是绝不能使工业瘫痪。如果工业陷入瘫痪，现代俄国所存在的种种恶果，或者同样巨大的其他恶果，在实践中似乎是不可避免的。将会出现城市与乡村的问题，将会出现饥荒，将会出现残暴、叛乱与军事专政。所有这些都沿着宿命的序列接踵而至；而它们的结局几乎肯定会与真正共产党员所渴望的目标大相径庭。

如果要使工业在整个共产主义革命期间得以存续，就必须满足一系列当前在任何地方都尚未满足的条件。为了明确起见，不妨设想一下如果明天英国爆发共产主义革命将会发生什么。美国会立即对与我们的所有贸易实施禁运。棉纺织工业将崩溃，使全国最具生产力的人口中大约五百万人陷入失业。粮食供应将变得不足，而且如果像预料的那样海军因军官的怠工破坏而采取敌对立场或陷入瘫痪，粮食供应将出现灾难性的中断。其结果是，除非发生反革命，否则大约一半的人口将在最初的十二个月内死亡。在这样的基础上，显然不可能建立起一个成功的共产主义国家。

===Original===

What applies to England applies, in one form or another, to the remaining countries of Europe. Italian and German Socialists are, many of them, in a revolutionary frame of mind and could, if they chose, raise formidable revolts. They are urged by Moscow to do so, but they realize that, if they did, England and America would starve them. France, for many reasons, dare not offend England and America beyond a point. Thus, in every country except America, a successful Communist revolution is impossible for economico-political reasons. America, being self-contained and strong, would be capable, so far as material conditions go, of achieving a successful revolution; but in America the psychological conditions are as yet adverse. There is no other civilized country where capitalism is so strong and revolutionary Socialism so weak as in America. At the present moment, therefore, though it is by no means impossible that Communist revolutions may occur all over the Continent, it is nearly certain that they cannot be successful in any real sense. They will have to begin by a war against America, and possibly England, by a paralysis of industry, by starvation, militarism and the whole attendant train of evils with which Russia has made us familiar.
That Communism, whenever and wherever it is adopted, will have to begin by fighting the bourgeoisie, is highly probable. The important question is not whether there is to be fighting, but how long and severe it is to be. A short war, in which Communism won a rapid and easy victory, would do little harm. It is long, bitter and doubtful wars that must be avoided if anything of what makes Communism desirable is to survive.
Two practical consequences flow from this conclusion: first, that nothing can succeed until America is either converted to Communism, or at any rate willing to remain neutral; secondly, that it is a mistake to attempt to inaugurate Communism in a country where the majority are hostile, or rather, where the active opponents are as strong as the active supporters, because in such a state of opinion a very severe civil war is likely to result. It is necessary to have a great body of opinion favourable to Communism, and a rather weak opposition, before a really successful Communist state can be introduced either by revolution or by more or less constitutional methods.

===Chinese===

适用于英国的情况，也以某种形式适用于欧洲其余国家。意大利和德国的社会主义者，许多人处于革命的心态中，只要他们愿意，就能够掀起可怕的起义。莫斯科（Moscow）敦促他们这么做，但他们意识到，如果他们真这么做，英国和美国就会让他们饿死。出于诸多原因，法国不敢在一定限度之外得罪英美两国。因此，在除美国之外的每一个国家，由于经济与政治的原因，成功的共产主义革命都是不可能的。美国由于自给自足且实力强大，就物质条件而言，有能力实现成功的革命；但在美国，心理条件迄今为止依然是不利的。在所有文明国家中，没有任何一个国家的资本主义像美国那样强大，而革命社会主义像美国那样软弱。因此在眼下这个关头，尽管在整个欧洲大陆爆发共产主义革命绝非不可能，但几乎可以肯定它们在任何真正的意义上都无法取得成功。它们必须以对美国、并可能对英国的战争为开端，以工业瘫痪、饥荒、军国主义以及俄国已让我们司空见惯的整套伴随恶果为开端。

共产主义无论在何时何地被采纳，都将不得不以同资产阶级作战为开端，这是极有可能的。重要的问题不在于是否会有战斗，而在于战斗将持续多久、何等激烈。一场由共产主义迅速而轻松取得胜利的短暂战争，几乎不会造成什么危害。必须避免的是漫长、残酷且胜负难料的战争——如果共产主义中任何令人向往的成分终要得以存留的话。

从这一结论中引出两个实际推论：第一，在美洲要么皈依共产主义、要么无论如何愿意保持中立之前，任何事情都不可能成功；第二，试图在多数人怀有敌意的国家开创共产主义是一个错误——或者更确切地说，在积极的反对者多于且强于积极支持者的国家开创共产主义是一个错误。在共产主义革命最初的破坏性阶段，必须依靠工人的积极热情来防止彻底的崩溃。这种热情必须足以使他们心甘情愿地容忍艰难困苦，并积极配合那些致力于维持生产运作的人。如果大多数工人在精神上处于被动状态，仅仅受少数人的强力推动，他们就会因缺乏粮食和燃料而陷入绝望，他们就会逃往乡村，把那些在思想上支配他们的人抛在身后，听任机器失修损坏。在这样的人口中，即便他们没有积极反对，工业也必然崩溃。必须有一大批人能够理解所面临的问题，并准备无私地劳作以克服这些困难。

===Original===

It may be assumed that when Communism is first introduced, the higher technical and business staff will side with the capitalists and attempt sabotage unless they have no hopes of a counterrevolution. For this reason it is very necessary that among wage-earners there should be as wide a diffusion as possible of technical and business education, so that they may be able immediately to take control of big complex industries. In this respect Russia was very badly off, whereas England and America would be much more fortunate.
Self-government in industry is, I believe, the road by which England can best approach Communism. I do not doubt that the railways and the mines, after a little practice, could be run more efficiently by the workers, from the point of view of production, than they are at present by the capitalists. The Bolsheviks oppose self-government in industry everywhere, because it has failed in Russia, and their national self-esteem prevents them from admitting that this is due to the backwardness of Russia. This is one of the respects in which they are misled by the assumption that Russia must be in all ways a model to the rest of the world. I would go so far as to say that the winning of self-government in such industries as railways and mining is an essential preliminary to complete Communism. In England, especially, this is the case. The Unions can command whatever technical skill they may require; they are politically powerful; the demand for self-government is one for which there is widespread sympathy, and could be much more with adequate propaganda; moreover (what is important with the British temperament) self-government can be brought about gradually, by stages in each trade, and by extension from one trade to another. Capitalists value two things, their power and their money; many individuals among them value only the money. It is wiser to concentrate first on the power, as is done by seeking self-government in industry without confiscation of capitalist incomes. By this means the capitalists are gradually turned into obvious drones, their active functions in industry become nil, and they can be ultimately dispossessed without dislocation and without the possibility of any successful struggle on their parts.

===Chinese===

可以假定，当共产主义最初引入时，高级技术与管理人员将站在资本家一边，除非他们对反革命不抱希望，否则会尝试怠工破坏。因此，在工薪劳动者中间尽可能广泛地普及技术与商业管理教育是非常必要的，以便他们能够立即接管大型复杂工业的控制权。在这方面俄国的处境非常糟糕，而英国和美国则要幸运得多。

我相信，工业自治（Self-government in industry）是英国走向共产主义的最佳途径。我毫不怀疑，铁路和煤矿经过少许实践后，从生产的角度来看，由工人运作可以比目前由资本家运作更加高效。布尔什维克在各地反对工业自治，因为这在俄国失败了，而他们的民族自尊心阻止他们承认这是由于俄国的落后所致。这是他们在认定俄国在各个方面都必须成为世界其他地方典范的假定下误入歧途的方面之一。我甚至可以说，在铁路和采矿等工业中赢得自治，是走向彻底共产主义必不可少的前提。在英国尤其如此。工会可以支配其可能需要的任何技术人才；他们在政治上实力强大；对自治的诉求是一项拥有广泛同情的诉求，通过充分的宣传还可以获得更多的同情；此外（这在英国人的性格中至关重要），自治可以逐步实现，通过在每个行业分阶段推进，以及从一个行业推广到另一个行业。资本家看重两样东西：他们的权力和他们的金钱；他们当中的许多人只看重金钱。首先专注于夺取权力是更为明智的，正如通过在不没收资本家收入的前提下寻求工业自治所做的那样。通过这种手段，资本家逐渐变成了显而易见的寄生虫，他们在工业中的积极职能降为零，他们最终可以在不引起混乱、且不可能进行任何成功抗争的情况下被剥夺所有权。

===Original===

Another advantage of proceeding by way of self-government is that it tends to prevent the Communist regime, when it comes, from having that truly terrible degree of centralization which now exists in Russia. The Russians have been forced to centralize, partly by the problems of the war, but more by the shortage of all kinds of skill. This has compelled the few competent men to attempt each to do the work of ten men, which has not proved satisfactory in spite of heroic efforts. The idea of democracy has become discredited as the result first of syndicalism, and then of Bolshevism. But there are two different things that may be meant by democracy: we may mean the system of Parliamentary government, or we may mean the participation of the people in affairs. The discredit of the former is largely deserved, and I have no desire to uphold Parliament as an ideal institution. But it is a great misfortune if, from a confusion of ideas, men come to think that, because Parliaments are imperfect, there is no reason why there should be self-government. The grounds for advocating self-government are very familiar: first, that no benevolent despot can be trusted to know or pursue the interests of his subjects; second, that the practice of self-government is the only effective method of political education; third, that it tends to place the preponderance of force on the side of the constitution, and thus to promote order and stable government. Other reasons could be found, but I think these are the chief. In Russia self-government has disappeared, except within the Communist Party. If it is not to disappear elsewhere during a Communist revolution, it is very desirable that there should exist already important industries competently administered by the workers themselves.
The Bolshevik philosophy is promoted very largely by despair of more gradual methods. But this despair is a mark of impatience, and is not really warranted by the facts. It is by no means impossible, in the near future, to secure self-government in British railways and mines by constitutional means. This is not the sort of measure which would bring into operation an American blockade or a civil war or any of the other catastrophic dangers that are to be feared from a full-fledged Communist revolution in the present international situation.

===Chinese===

通过自治途径推进的另一个优势在于，当共产主义政权到来时，它倾向于防止出现俄国目前所存在的真正可怕程度的中央集权。俄国人被迫实行中央集权，部分是由于战争问题，但更多的是由于各种专门技能人才的短缺。这迫使少数有胜任能力的人每个人都试图承担十个人的工作，尽管付出了英雄般的努力，事实证明这并不尽如人意。民主的观念首先由于工团主义、继而由于布尔什维主义而声名狼藉。但民主可以有两种不同的含义：我们可以指议会制政府的体制，也可以指人民对事务的参与。前者的声名扫地在很大程度上是咎由自取，我毫无将议会作为理想制度来维护的愿望。但如果由于观念的混淆，人们开始认为既然议会是不完善的就没有理由实行自治，那将是一大不幸。倡导自治的理由是人所共知的：第一，不能指望任何仁慈的专制君主能够了解或追求其臣民的利益；第二，自治的实践是进行政治教育的唯一有效方法；第三，它倾向于将优势力量置于宪法一边，从而促进秩序与稳定的治理。还可以找到其他理由，但我认为这些是主要的。在俄国，除了在共产党内部，自治已经荡然无存。如果不想在共产主义革命期间在其他地方也消失殆尽，那么极其理想的是，届时应当已经存在由工人自己称职管理的重要工业。

布尔什维克哲学在很大程度上是由对渐进方法的绝望所促成的。但这种绝望是不耐烦的标志，事实上并没有充足的根据。在不久的将来，通过宪政手段在英国铁路和煤矿中实现自治绝非不可能。这并不是那种会招致美国封锁、内战或在当前国际局势下由于全面的共产主义革命所令人畏惧的任何其他灾难性危险的举措。

===Original===

Self-government in industry is feasible, and would be a great step towards Communism. It would both afford many of the advantages of Communism and also make the transition far easier without a technical breakdown of production.
There is another defect in the methods advocated by the Third International. The sort of revolution which is recommended is never practically feasible except in a time of national misfortune; in fact, defeat in war seems to be an indispensable condition. Consequently, by this method, Communism will only be inaugurated where the conditions of life are difficult, where demoralization and disorganization make success almost impossible, and where men are in a mood of fierce despair very inimical to industrial construction. If Communism is to have a fair chance, it must be inaugurated in a prosperous country. But a prosperous country will not be readily moved by the arguments of hatred and universal upheaval which are employed by the Third International. It is necessary, in appealing to a prosperous country, to lay stress on hope rather than despair, and to show how the transition can be effected without a calamitous loss of prosperity. All this requires less violence and subversiveness, more patience and constructive propaganda, less appeal to the armed might of a determined minority.
The attitude of uncompromising heroism is attractive, and appeals especially to the dramatic instinct. But the purpose of the serious revolutionary is not personal heroism, nor martyrdom, but the creation of a happier world. Those who have the happiness of the world at heart will shrink from attitudes and the facile hysteria of “no parley with the enemy.” They will not embark upon enterprises, however arduous and austere, which are likely to involve the martyrdom of their country and the discrediting of their ideals. It is by slower and less showy methods that the new world must be built: by industrial efforts after self-government, by proletarian training in technique and business administration, by careful study of the international situation, by a prolonged and devoted propaganda of ideas rather than tactics, especially among the wage-earners of the United States. It is not true that no gradual approaches to Communism are possible: self-government in industry is an important instance to the contrary.

===Chinese===

工业自治是可行的，而且将是迈向共产主义的一大步。它不仅能提供共产主义的诸多优势，还能使过渡变得容易得多，而不会发生生产上的技术性崩溃。

在第三国际所倡导的方法中还存在另一个缺陷。它所推荐的那种革命，除了在国家蒙难时期之外，在实践中绝非切实可行；事实上，战败似乎是一个不可或缺的条件。因此，通过这种方法，共产主义将唯独在生活条件艰难、士气涣散和组织失序使成功几乎毫无可能、且人们处于极不利于工业建设的凶狠绝望情绪之中的地方才能开启。如果要使共产主义拥有公平的机会，它就必须在一个繁荣的国家中开启。但是一个繁荣的国家是绝不会轻易为第三国际所采用的仇恨与普遍大动荡的论调所打动的。在向一个繁荣国家发出呼吁时，必须强调希望而非绝望，并且必须表明如何在不造成灾难性繁荣损失的情况下实现过渡。所有这一切都需要更少的暴力与颠覆性，更多的耐心与建设性宣传，更少诉诸坚定少数派的武装力量。

毫不妥协的英雄主义姿态是诱人的，尤其契合戏剧性的本能。但严肃革命者的目的不是个人英雄主义，亦非殉道，而是创造一个更幸福的世界。那些把世界的幸福放在心上的人，将对摆出姿态以及“绝不与敌人妥协”的轻率歇斯底里退避三舍。他们绝不会去从事那些无论何等艰苦和严峻、却可能导致其国家殉道并使其理想名誉扫地的事业。必须通过更缓慢且不那么张扬的方法来建设新世界：通过争取自治的工业努力，通过对无产阶级在技术和商业管理上的培训，通过对国际局势的细致研究，通过旷日持久且执着投入的观念宣传而非策略宣传，尤其是在美国工薪劳动者中间开展宣传。所谓不可能逐步走向共产主义的说法是不符合事实的：工业自治就是一个证明相反情况的重要实例。

===Original===

It is not true that any isolated European country, or even the whole of the Continent in unison, can, after the exhaustion produced by the war, introduce a successful form of Communism at the present moment, owing to the hostility and economic supremacy of America. To find fault with those who urge these considerations, or to accuse them of faint-heartedness, is mere sentimental self-indulgence, sacrificing the good we can do to the satisfaction of our own emotions.
Even under present conditions in Russia, it is possible still to feel the inspiration of the essential spirit of Communism, the spirit of creative hope, seeking to sweep away the incumbrances of injustice and tyranny and rapacity which obstruct the growth of the human spirit, to replace individual competition by collective action, the relation of master and slave by free cooperation. This hope has helped the best of the Communists to bear the harsh years through which Russia has been passing, and has become an inspiration to the world. The hope is not chimerical, but it can only be realized through a more patient labour, a more objective study of facts, and above all a longer propaganda, to make the necessity of the transition obvious to the great majority of wage-earners. Russian Communism may fail and go under, but Communism itself will not die. And if hope rather than hatred inspires its advocates, it can be brought about without the universal cataclysm preached by Moscow. The war and its sequel have proved the destructiveness of capitalism; let us see to it that the next epoch does not prove the still greater destructiveness of Communism, but rather its power to heal the wounds which the old evil system has inflicted upon the human spirit.

===Chinese===

所谓任何孤立的欧洲国家、甚至整个欧洲大陆步调一致地在经历了战争所致的精疲力竭之后，能够在眼下这个关头引入成功的共产主义形态的说法，鉴于美国的敌意和经济霸权，也是不符合事实的。责难那些力陈这些考量的人，或者指责他们懦弱胆怯，不过是纯粹感伤主义的自我放纵，为了满足我们自身的情感而牺牲了我们所能做出的善行。

即便在俄国当前的条件下，人们依然可能感受到共产主义本质精神的激励——那种创造性希望的精神，它寻求扫除阻碍人类精神成长的不公、暴政与贪婪的累赘，用集体行动取代个人竞争，用自由合作取代主奴关系。正是这种希望帮助了最优秀的共产党员熬过了俄国正在经历的严酷岁月，并成为全世界的灵感源泉。这种希望并非空中楼阁，但它唯有通过更加耐心的劳作、对事实更加客观的研究、最重要的是通过更加长期的宣传，使过渡的必要性对广大工薪劳动者变得显而易见，才有可能得以实现。俄国共产主义可能会失败并垮台，但共产主义本身是不会消亡的。而且如果激励其拥护者的是希望而非仇恨，它就能够在不发生莫斯科所宣扬的普遍浩劫的情况下得以实现。战争及其余波已经证明了资本主义的毁灭性；让我们确保下一个时代不要证明共产主义更加巨大的毁灭性，而是证明其医治旧有罪恶制度给人类精神所造成的创伤的力量。

## Endnotes / 尾注
===Original===

1. See the article “On the role of the Communist Party in the Proletarian Revolution,” in *Theses Presented to the Second Congress of the Communist International*, Petrograd-Moscow, 18 July, 1920﻿—a valuable work which I possess only in French. ↩︎
2. Electrification is desired not merely for reorganizing industry, but in order to industrialize agriculture. In *Theses Presented to the Second Congress of the Communist International* (an instructive little book, which I shall quote as *Theses*), it is said in an article on the Agrarian question that Socialism will not be secure till industry is reorganized on a new basis with “general application of electric energy in all branches of agriculture and rural economy,” which “alone can give to the towns the possibility of offering to backward rural districts a technical and social aid capable of determining an extraordinary increase of productivity of agricultural and rural labour, and of engaging the small cultivators, in their own interest, to pass progressively to a collectivist mechanical cultivation” (p. 36 of French edition). ↩︎
3. In *Theses* (p. 34) it is said: “It would be an irreparable error﻿ ﻿… not to admit the gratuitous grant of part of the expropriated lands to poor and even well-to-do peasants.” ↩︎
4. In *Theses* (p. 6 of French edition) it is said:

===Chinese===

1. 参见收入《提交共产国际第二次代表大会（Second Congress of the Communist International）的决议提纲》（彼得格勒〔Petrograd〕—莫斯科〔Moscow〕，1920 年 7 月 18 日）中的文章《论共产党在无产阶级革命中的作用》（“On the role of the Communist Party in the Proletarian Revolution”）——这是一部非常有价值的文献，但我手头只有法文版。↩︎
2. 推行电气化不仅是为了改组工业，也是为了实现农业的工业化。在《提交共产国际第二次代表大会的决议提纲》（一本颇具教益的小册子，我将简称为《提纲》）中，一篇论述土地问题的文章谈道：除非工业在新的基础上重组，并在“农业和农村经济的所有部门中普遍应用电能”，否则社会主义将无法稳固建立；而这种应用“唯有它才能使城市有可能向落后的农村地区提供技术和社会援助，从而促成农业和农村劳动生产率的非凡提高，并促使小农为了自身利益逐步过渡到集体化的机械耕作”（法文版第 36 页）。↩︎
3. 在《提纲》（第 34 页）中谈道：“不承认将一部分被剥夺的土地无偿分配给贫农甚至富裕农民，将是一个无法挽回的错误……”↩︎
4. 在《提纲》（法文版第 6 页）中谈道：

===Original===

The ancient classic subdivision of the Labour movement into three forms (parties, trade unions, and cooperatives) has served its time. The proletarian revolution has raised up in Russia the essential form of proletarian dictatorship, the soviets. But the work in the Soviets, as in the industrial trade unions which have become revolutionary, must be invariably and systematically directed by the party of the proletariat, i.e. the Communist Party. As the organized advanced guard of the working class, the Communist Party answers equally to the economic, political and spiritual needs of the entire working class. It must be the soul of the trade unions, the soviets, and all other proletarian organizations.
 The appearance of the Soviets, the principal historical form of the dictatorship of the proletariat, in no way diminishes the directing role of the party in the proletarian revolution. When the German Communists of the “Left”﻿ ﻿… declare that “the party itself must also adapt itself more and more to the Soviet idea and proletarianize itself,” we see there only an insinuating expression of the idea that the Communist Party must dissolve itself into the Soviets, so that the Soviets can replace it.
 This idea is profoundly erroneous and reactionary.
 The history of the Russian Revolution shows us, at a certain moment, the Soviets going against the proletarian party and helping the agents of the bourgeoisie.﻿ ﻿…
 In order that the Soviets may fulfil their historic mission, the existence of a Communist Party, strong enough not to “adapt” itself to the Soviets but to exercise on them a decisive influence, to force them *not to adapt themselves* to the bourgeoisie and official social democracy,﻿ ﻿… is on the contrary necessary.

===Chinese===

工人运动将自身划分为三种形式（政党、工会〔trade unions〕与合作社〔cooperatives〕）的古老经典划分法已经过时了。无产阶级革命在俄国树立了无产阶级专政（dictatorship of the proletariat）的基本形式——苏维埃（Soviet）。但是，在苏维埃内部的工作，正如在已经变为革命性的产业工会中的工作一样，必须始终一贯、有系统地由无产阶级政党即共产党来领导。作为工人阶级有组织的先锋队，共产党同样回应着整个工人阶级在经济、政治和精神上的需求。它必须成为工会、苏维埃以及所有其他无产阶级组织的灵魂。
 苏维埃这一无产阶级专政主要历史形式的出现，绝没有削弱党在无产阶级革命中的领导作用。当德国“左派”共产党员……声称“党本身也必须越来越适应苏维埃思想并实现自身的无产阶级化”时，我们在其中看到的不过是一种拐弯抹角的表达，其实质是主张共产党必须融入苏维埃之中，以便让苏维埃取而代之。
 这种观点是极其错误且反动的。
 俄国革命（Russian Revolution）的历史向我们表明，在某一特定时刻，苏维埃曾与无产阶级政党背道而驰，并帮助了资产阶级的代理人……
 为了使苏维埃能够完成其历史使命，必须存在一个足够强大的共产党，它不仅不去“适应”苏维埃，反而能够对苏维埃施加决定性影响，迫使它们*不让自己去适应*资产阶级和官方的社会民主党……相反，这才是绝对必要的。

===Original===

↩︎
5. The ninth Communist Congress (March﻿–﻿April, 1920) says on this subject: “In view of the fact that the first condition of the success of the Soviet Republic in all departments, including the economic, is chiefly systematic printed agitation, the Congress draws the attention of the Soviet Government to the deplorable state in which our paper and printing industries find themselves. The ever decreasing number of newspapers fail to reach not only the peasants but even the workers, in addition to which our poor technical means render the papers hardly readable. The Congress strongly appeals to the Supreme Council of Public Economy, to the corresponding Trade Unions and other interested institutions, to apply all efforts to raise the quantity, to introduce general system and order in the printing business, and so secure for the worker and peasant in Russia a supply of Socialist printed matter.” ↩︎

===Chinese===

↩︎
5. 俄共第九次代表大会（1920 年 3—4 月）在这个问题上指出：“鉴于苏维埃共和国在包括经济部门在内的一切领域取得成功的首要条件主要是系统化的印刷品鼓动，大会提请苏维埃政府注意我国造纸和印刷工业所陷入的悲惨境地。日益减少的报纸不仅无法送达农民手中，甚至连工人都看不到，此外我们拙劣的技术手段导致报纸几乎难以辨读。大会强烈呼吁最高国民经济委员会（Supreme Council of Public Economy）、相应的工会以及其他相关机构竭尽全力提高印刷数量，在印刷行业引入普遍的体制与秩序，从而确保俄国的工人和农民能够获得社会主义印刷品供应。” ↩︎
